- [ ] **Step 1: Η γεννήτρια του dry run**

`worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073-dryrun.mjs`:
```js
// Generates the 073 dry run FROM its source:
//   073_plan_money.sql -> 073_plan_money_dryrun.sql
//   node worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073-dryrun.mjs          (writes it)
//   node worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073-dryrun.mjs --check  (exit 1 if the committed file drifted)
// Why generated, never hand-copied (principle 3): a dry run is only worth anything if it runs EXACTLY the
// statements the owner will run. tests/sql-073-drafts.test.js fails when the committed dry run differs.
// Shape (as 072): ONE DO $dry$ block - 073's DO block VERBATIM as a nested block (its guards and proofs raise their
// own messages and stop everything), a read of what it did, and a final RAISE EXCEPTION that carries the result
// line and undoes all of it (pg_notify included: a NOTIFY is sent only at COMMIT). ASCII in, ASCII out.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
// The folder that holds the 073 files. After the owner runs them they leave drafts/ (CLAUDE.md rule 5): then
// this becomes path.resolve(HERE, '../..') and nothing else changes.
export const DIR = path.resolve(HERE, '..');
const OPEN = '\nDO $do$\n';
const CLOSE = '\nEND $do$;\n';

export const PAIR = {
  source: '073_plan_money.sql', target: '073_plan_money_dryrun.sql', name: 'DRY RUN 073',
  header: `-- 073 DRY RUN - GENERATED by worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073-dryrun.mjs from 073_plan_money.sql.
-- DO NOT EDIT BY HAND: edit the source and regenerate (tests/sql-073-drafts.test.js fails on any drift).
-- WHY ONE DO BLOCK: the Supabase SQL editor is NOT atomic across statements (lesson 056), but ONE DO block is
--   ONE statement: its final error undoes everything inside it. ASCII only.
-- WHAT: 073's own DO block, statement for statement (the guards, the helper, the 5 hooks in 072, the 7 views, the
--   index, the rights, the before = after proof), then a read of the result, ENDING WITH A DELIBERATE ERROR:
--   NOTHING is kept.
-- WHEN: AFTER 15:00 (the team works 05:30-14:30), AFTER 072 ran, BEFORE 073_plan_money.sql. Same 5 s lock timeout.
-- EXPECTED (the red ERROR is deliberate): a NOTICE "073 OK: ..." and then
--   ERROR: P0001: DRY RUN 073 finished - EVERYTHING UNDONE, nothing kept. The block ran to its end  ||  073 ran:
--   v5 revenue view yes, helper yes, stop index yes, pnl rows <as before>, route rows <as before>, v5 rows 0, triggers <B-54>
--   Anything else (an ERROR from one of 073's guards or proofs): do NOT run 073, copy the message to the coordinator.`,
  epilogue: `  res := format('073 ran: v5 revenue view %s, helper %s, stop index %s, pnl rows %s, route rows %s, v5 rows %s, triggers %s',
    CASE WHEN to_regclass('public.ct_v_v5_revenue') IS NOT NULL THEN 'yes' ELSE 'NO' END,
    CASE WHEN to_regprocedure('public.plan_h_pallet_sync(bigint[],text,text)') IS NOT NULL THEN 'yes' ELSE 'NO' END,
    CASE WHEN to_regclass('public.pl_mov_stop_once') IS NOT NULL THEN 'yes' ELSE 'NO' END,
    (SELECT count(*) FROM public.ct_v_rt_pnl), (SELECT count(*) FROM public.dl_v_rt_route),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM public.shipments) + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.shipment_pieces)
      + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.rt_stops) + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.piece_legs)
      + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.plan_notices) + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.pl_movements WHERE piece_leg_id IS NOT NULL),
    (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_trigger t JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = t.tgrelid JOIN pg_namespace ns ON ns.oid = c.relnamespace
      WHERE NOT t.tgisinternal AND t.tgenabled <> 'D' AND ns.nspname = 'public'));`,
};

export function buildDryRun(src, pair = PAIR) {
  const a = src.indexOf(OPEN);
  const b = src.lastIndexOf(CLOSE);
  if (a < 0 || b < 0 || b < a) throw new Error(`${pair.source}: the DO $do$ ... END $do$; block was not found`);
  const body = src.slice(a + OPEN.length, b);   // "DECLARE ... BEGIN ... " of the source, verbatim
  if (body.includes('$dry$')) throw new Error(`${pair.source}: contains $dry$ - choose other tags`);
  return `${pair.header}

DO $dry$
DECLARE
  res text;
BEGIN
  -- ===== ${pair.source}, verbatim =====
${body}
END;
  -- ===== what it did =====
${pair.epilogue}
  RAISE EXCEPTION '${pair.name} finished - EVERYTHING UNDONE, nothing kept. The block ran to its end  ||  %', res;
END
$dry$;
`;
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  const check = process.argv.includes('--check');
  const source = path.join(DIR, PAIR.source);
  if (!fs.existsSync(source)) { console.error('no source: ' + PAIR.source); process.exit(1); }
  const out = buildDryRun(fs.readFileSync(source, 'utf8'));
  const target = path.join(DIR, PAIR.target);
  if (check) {
    const same = fs.existsSync(target) && fs.readFileSync(target, 'utf8') === out;
    if (!same) console.error('drift: ' + PAIR.target);
    process.exit(same ? 0 : 1);
  }
  fs.writeFileSync(target, out);
  console.log('wrote ' + PAIR.target);
}
```

Run: `node worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073-dryrun.mjs && node worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073-dryrun.mjs --check && echo DRYRUN-IDIO`
Expected: `wrote 073_plan_money_dryrun.sql` και `DRYRUN-IDIO`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Verify (μόνο SELECT)**

`worker/migrations/drafts/073_plan_money_verify.sql`:
```sql
-- 073 VERIFY - read-only (SELECT only, writes nothing). Run after 073_plan_money.sql and any time later.
-- ONE row. ASCII only. Works before 073 too (then applied = '073 NOT in place').
-- EXPECTED right after 073 (and until day L): applied = '073 in place' ; views_changed = 0 ; functions_changed = 0 ;
--   invoker_lost = 0 ; open_money = 0 ; open_helper = 0 ; stop_once = true ; b54_matches = true ; v5_rows = 0.
-- After L: v5_rows grows; every other value stays as above for ever (a change = someone rewrote a view or a function).
SELECT
  CASE WHEN to_regprocedure('public.plan_h_pallet_sync(bigint[],text,text)') IS NOT NULL
        AND to_regclass('public.ct_v_v5_revenue') IS NOT NULL AND to_regclass('public.pl_mov_stop_once') IS NOT NULL
       THEN '073 in place' ELSE '073 NOT in place' END AS applied,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM (VALUES
      ('public.ct_v_v5_revenue', '3005a46eccbd73fb569c2441bdd959ee'),
      ('public.ct_v_rt_revenue', '6dc1e56a824f285203a2613d6231233a'),
      ('public.ct_v_rt_costs', 'ffd8d8191117b0833fb656faa10d3fa2'),
      ('public.ct_v_rt_pnl', '26e49204998a386c22f45ebf1746b177'),
      ('public.dl_v_rt_route', 'b21405a7856b912d2ea697235461cbd6'),
      ('public.ct_v_rt_pallet_gate', '92daf0df9d41c2028880fac7be0167c5'),
      ('public.stock_v_pieces', 'db95036d7dcc8cb02d32843ee0b2fb7c')) g(rel, m)
    WHERE to_regclass(g.rel) IS NULL OR md5(pg_get_viewdef(to_regclass(g.rel), true)) <> g.m) AS views_changed,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM (VALUES
      ('public.plan_h_leg_add(bigint,bigint,bigint,bigint,text,text,integer)', '0d95758b35f6095d42b551c287e3008b'),
      ('public.plan_h_leg_drop(bigint,text,text,text)', 'cb48e1aeba2d8e78191b9fc47eae6d67'),
      ('public.plan_h_drop_consequences(bigint[],bigint,bigint[])', '3218280047f5b3e0aef78b1dd56d989e'),
      ('public.plan_order_update(jsonb,text,text)', '76c6d0bd9fe721ea19d2dd2547997131'),
      ('public.plan_rt_update(jsonb,text,text)', '4fb0a4297bd07b74f7c73face7b60637')) f(sig, m)
    WHERE to_regprocedure(f.sig) IS NULL OR (SELECT md5(prosrc) FROM pg_proc WHERE oid = to_regprocedure(f.sig)) <> f.m) AS functions_changed,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_class c WHERE c.oid IN ('public.dl_v_rt_route'::regclass, 'public.ct_v_rt_pallet_gate'::regclass)
     AND c.reloptions IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['security_invoker=true']) AS invoker_lost,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM unnest(ARRAY['anon', 'authenticated', 'tms_reader']) r(role)
    WHERE to_regclass('public.ct_v_v5_revenue') IS NOT NULL AND has_table_privilege(r.role, 'public.ct_v_v5_revenue', 'SELECT')) AS open_money,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM unnest(ARRAY['anon', 'authenticated', 'service_role']) r(role)
    WHERE to_regprocedure('public.plan_h_pallet_sync(bigint[],text,text)') IS NOT NULL
      AND has_function_privilege(r.role, 'public.plan_h_pallet_sync(bigint[],text,text)', 'EXECUTE')) AS open_helper,
  EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND indexname = 'pl_mov_stop_once'
           AND indexdef LIKE '%UNIQUE%(order_stop_id, event_type)%') AS stop_once,
  (SELECT red_value FROM monitoring.checks WHERE id = 'B-54') = (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_trigger t JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = t.tgrelid
     JOIN pg_namespace ns ON ns.oid = c.relnamespace WHERE NOT t.tgisinternal AND t.tgenabled <> 'D' AND ns.nspname = 'public') AS b54_matches,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM public.shipments) + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.shipment_pieces) + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.rt_stops)
    + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.piece_legs) + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.plan_notices)
    + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.pl_movements WHERE piece_leg_id IS NOT NULL) AS v5_rows,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM public.ct_v_rt_pnl) AS pnl_rows,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM public.dl_v_rt_route) AS route_rows;
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Αποτύπωμα (μόνο SELECT, πριν και μετά)**

`worker/migrations/drafts/073_plan_money_fingerprint.sql`:
```sql
-- 073 FINGERPRINT - read-only. Run it BEFORE 073 and right AFTER 073 (and after the rollback): the same row, if nobody
-- wrote in between (the content depends on today's date: run both the same evening). It holds every view 073 replaces
-- and every view that reads them, as ordered rows (the new last column of ct_v_rt_pnl is left out).
SELECT now() AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens' AS athens_now,
  (SELECT count(*) FROM public.ct_round_trips) AS round_trips,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.rt_id), '')) FROM public.ct_v_rt_revenue v) AS revenue,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.rt_id), '')) FROM public.ct_v_rt_costs v) AS costs,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg((to_jsonb(v) - 'partner_rate_missing')::text, '|' ORDER BY v.id), '')) FROM public.ct_v_rt_pnl v) AS pnl,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.rt_id), '')) FROM public.dl_v_rt_route v) AS route,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.id), '')) FROM public.dl_v_entries v) AS dl_entries_view,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.driver_id), '')) FROM public.dl_v_balance v) AS dl_balance,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.rt_id), '')) FROM public.ct_v_rt_pallet_gate v) AS pallet_gate,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.piece_kind, v.piece_id), '')) FROM public.stock_v_pieces v) AS stock_pieces,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.lot_id), '')) FROM public.stock_v_lot_money v) AS lot_money,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(m)::text, '|' ORDER BY m.id), '')) FROM public.pl_movements m) AS pallets,
  (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(e)::text, '|' ORDER BY e.id), '')) FROM public.dl_entries e) AS payroll;
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Rollback**

Επαναφέρει τα κείμενα της 23/10 (με `U&''` για τα βέλη: η όψη ξαναβγαίνει με το ίδιο md5 `cfb5f85b…`, δοκιμάστηκε). Το `ct_v_rt_pnl` σβήνεται και ξαναγίνεται (το `CREATE OR REPLACE` δεν βγάζει στήλη)· κανένα αντικείμενο δεν εξαρτάται από αυτό (μετρήθηκε 23/10) και τα δικαιώματά του ξαναδίνονται από τα δικά του (`aclexplode`), με απόδειξη.

`worker/migrations/drafts/073_plan_money_rollback.sql`:
```sql
-- 073 ROLLBACK - puts back exactly what 073 changed: the 5 functions of 072 (their 072 text, byte for byte), the 6 views
-- (their 23/10 text, the same rows, options and rights), and removes ct_v_v5_revenue, plan_h_pallet_sync and
-- pl_mov_stop_once. Only with an explicit yes, after 15:00. Run it BEFORE 072's rollback (that one drops every plan_*).
-- It REFUSES as soon as one v5 row exists (spec 2.2: after the first real v5 row there is no going back, only fixes
-- forward; with the /plan/* writes closed until day L there is none before L).
-- ASCII only (the arrows of the route are U&'' escapes). ONE DO block = ONE statement: an error anywhere undoes all of it.
-- EXPECTED: NOTICE "073 ROLLBACK OK ..." and ONE row "073 ROLLED BACK | <the 23/10 md5 of ct_v_rt_revenue> | ...".

DO $do$
DECLARE
  snap_sql CONSTANT text := $q$
    SELECT concat_ws(' ',
      (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.rt_id), '')) FROM public.ct_v_rt_revenue v),
      (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.rt_id), '')) FROM public.ct_v_rt_costs v),
      (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg((to_jsonb(v) - 'partner_rate_missing')::text, '|' ORDER BY v.id), '')) FROM public.ct_v_rt_pnl v),
      (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.rt_id), '')) FROM public.dl_v_rt_route v),
      (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.rt_id), '')) FROM public.ct_v_rt_pallet_gate v),
      (SELECT count(*) || ':' || md5(coalesce(string_agg(to_jsonb(v)::text, '|' ORDER BY v.piece_kind, v.piece_id), '')) FROM public.stock_v_pieces v)) $q$;
  v_g record; v_src text; v_new text; v_def text; v_k bigint; v_snap0 text; v_snap1 text; v_acl0 text; v_acl1 text;
  v_grants text[];
BEGIN
  PERFORM set_config('lock_timeout', '5s', true);
  IF to_regprocedure('public.plan_h_pallet_sync(bigint[],text,text)') IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073 rollback: 073 is not applied - nothing to undo';
  END IF;
  SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM public.shipments) + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.shipment_pieces)
       + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.rt_stops) + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.piece_legs)
       + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.plan_notices) + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.ct_round_trips WHERE model = 2)
       + (SELECT count(*) FROM public.pl_movements WHERE piece_leg_id IS NOT NULL)
    INTO v_k;
  IF v_k <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073 rollback: % v5 row(s) exist - after the first real v5 row there is no rollback, only fixes forward (spec 2.2)', v_k;
  END IF;
  EXECUTE snap_sql INTO v_snap0;
  -- the rights of ct_v_rt_pnl, the one view that must be dropped (CREATE OR REPLACE cannot remove its new last column)
  SELECT coalesce(array_agg(format('GRANT %s ON public.ct_v_rt_pnl TO %s', a.privilege_type,
                                   CASE WHEN a.grantee = 0 THEN 'PUBLIC' ELSE quote_ident(pg_get_userbyid(a.grantee)) END)
                            ORDER BY a.grantee, a.privilege_type), '{}'),
         (SELECT relacl::text FROM pg_class WHERE oid = 'public.ct_v_rt_pnl'::regclass)
    INTO v_grants, v_acl0
    FROM pg_class c CROSS JOIN LATERAL aclexplode(c.relacl) a
   WHERE c.oid = 'public.ct_v_rt_pnl'::regclass AND a.grantee <> c.relowner;

  -- 1. 072's five functions: the hook out, from the LIVE text (073's), checked against 072's md5
  FOR v_g IN SELECT * FROM (VALUES
      ('public.plan_h_leg_add(bigint,bigint,bigint,bigint,text,text,integer)',
       E'  -- 073 (E11): the pallet feeder of this leg (pl_movements.piece_leg_id), in the same transaction.\n'
       || E'  perform plan_h_pallet_sync(array[l.id], p_actor, p_role);\n',
       E'  -- F3 hook: the pallet feeder of this leg (pl_movements.piece_leg_id, 073) is written here.\n',
       '0d95758b35f6095d42b551c287e3008b', 'aa9645fa495976bbca7c7de0635f4c22'),
      ('public.plan_h_leg_drop(bigint,text,text,text)',
       E'  perform plan_h_audit(p_actor, p_role, ''delete'', ''piece_legs'', p_leg::text, to_jsonb(a), to_jsonb(b));\n'
       || E'  -- 073 (E11): what this leg wrote in the pallet ledger is withdrawn with it (never deleted)\n'
       || E'  perform plan_h_pallet_sync(array[p_leg], p_actor, p_role);\n',
       E'  perform plan_h_audit(p_actor, p_role, ''delete'', ''piece_legs'', p_leg::text, to_jsonb(a), to_jsonb(b));\n',
       'cb48e1aeba2d8e78191b9fc47eae6d67', '4533385f7284c9ba5af52cf585a03250'),
      ('public.plan_h_drop_consequences(bigint[],bigint,bigint[])',
       E'  -- 073 (E11, D-8): a confirmed pallet row of a leg that goes is withdrawn after the token - listed first\n'
       || E'  v := v || coalesce((select jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(''kind'', ''pallets_confirmed'', ''leg_id'', m.piece_leg_id,\n'
       || E'                                                         ''pl_movement_id'', m.id, ''event_type'', m.event_type,\n'
       || E'                                                         ''taken'', m.taken, ''given'', m.given))\n'
       || E'                        from pl_movements m join piece_legs l on l.id = m.piece_leg_id\n'
       || E'                       where l.id = any (p_legs) and l.deleted_at is null and m.status = ''confirmed''), ''[]''::jsonb);\n'
       || E'  for r in select distinct l.rt_id from piece_legs l\n',
       E'  for r in select distinct l.rt_id from piece_legs l\n',
       '3218280047f5b3e0aef78b1dd56d989e', '1dc5f3d8da4939a2ed6137621b5b9bfd'),
      ('public.plan_order_update(jsonb,text,text)',
       E'  -- 073 (E11): the pallet rows of this order''s legs follow the correction (pallets, pallet exchange)\n'
       || E'  perform plan_h_pallet_sync(array(select l.id from piece_legs l join shipment_pieces sp on sp.id = l.piece_id\n'
       || E'                                     join shipments sh on sh.id = sp.shipment_id\n'
       || E'                                    where sh.nat_order_id = n.id and l.deleted_at is null), p_actor, p_role);\n'
       || E'  perform plan_h_finish();\n  return plan_h_ok(''plan_order_update''',
       E'  perform plan_h_finish();\n  return plan_h_ok(''plan_order_update''',
       '76c6d0bd9fe721ea19d2dd2547997131', 'bf69fe4bfc46730ed53b0e850d172d9e'),
      ('public.plan_rt_update(jsonb,text,text)',
       E'    -- 073 (E11, flows gap 11): the pending pallet rows of the cycle follow its stop days\n'
       || E'    perform plan_h_pallet_sync(array(select l.id from piece_legs l where l.rt_id = v_rt and l.deleted_at is null),\n'
       || E'                               p_actor, p_role);\n'
       || E'    -- 6. the international day follows the cycle that carries the first piece (decision 16)',
       E'    -- 6. the international day follows the cycle that carries the first piece (decision 16)',
       '4fb0a4297bd07b74f7c73face7b60637', 'ed90048867c25110d8dee3cf6400aca1')
    ) v(sig, a, b, m073, m072) LOOP
    SELECT prosrc INTO v_src FROM pg_proc WHERE oid = to_regprocedure(v_g.sig);
    IF md5(v_src) <> v_g.m073 THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION '073 rollback: % is not the text 073 wrote (md5 %) - stop', v_g.sig, md5(v_src);
    END IF;
    v_new := replace(v_src, v_g.a, v_g.b);
    IF md5(v_new) <> v_g.m072 THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION '073 rollback: the 072 text of % does not come back (md5 %) - stop', v_g.sig, md5(v_new);
    END IF;
    v_def := pg_get_functiondef(to_regprocedure(v_g.sig));
    EXECUTE replace(v_def, v_src, v_new);
  END LOOP;

  -- 2. the views, back to their 23/10 text (the revenue first: it stops reading ct_v_v5_revenue)
  CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.ct_v_rt_revenue AS
 WITH natord_home AS (
         SELECT nord.id AS national_order_id,
            nord.price,
            COALESCE(( SELECT min(h1.id) AS min
                   FROM national_loads h1
                  WHERE h1.source_national_order_id = nord.id AND h1.deleted_at IS NULL), ( SELECT min(h2.id) AS min
                   FROM national_loads h2
                  WHERE h2.deleted_at IS NULL AND h2.source_cons_load_id = (( SELECT gl.cons_load_id
                           FROM groupage_lines gl
                          WHERE gl.national_order_id = nord.id AND gl.deleted_at IS NULL AND gl.cons_load_id IS NOT NULL
                          ORDER BY gl.id
                         LIMIT 1)))) AS home_nat_load_id
           FROM national_orders nord
          WHERE nord.deleted_at IS NULL AND nord.price IS NOT NULL AND nord.source_order_id IS NULL
        ), legs AS (
         SELECT rt.id AS rt_id,
                CASE
                    WHEN l.order_id IS NOT NULL THEN COALESCE(sa.rt_amount, o.price,
                    CASE
                        WHEN o.leg_no = 1 THEN p.price
                        ELSE NULL::numeric
                    END, 0::numeric) -
                    CASE
                        WHEN
                        CASE
                            WHEN o.parent_order_id IS NOT NULL THEN p.veroia_switch
                            ELSE o.veroia_switch
                        END THEN stock_vs_charge(
                        CASE
                            WHEN
                            CASE
                                WHEN o.parent_order_id IS NOT NULL THEN p.direction
                                ELSE o.direction
                            END = 'Export'::text THEN ct_setting('x_export'::text)
                            ELSE ct_setting('x_import'::text)
                        END, vp.pallets)
                        ELSE 0::numeric
                    END
                    ELSE 0::numeric
                END AS rev_intl,
                CASE
                    WHEN l.nat_load_id IS NOT NULL THEN
                    CASE
                        WHEN nl.source_type = 'Direct'::text AND nl.source_order_id IS NOT NULL AND nl.id = (( SELECT min(d.id) AS min
                           FROM national_loads d
                          WHERE d.source_order_id = nl.source_order_id AND d.source_type = 'Direct'::text AND d.deleted_at IS NULL)) AND
                        CASE
                            WHEN so.parent_order_id IS NOT NULL THEN sp.veroia_switch
                            ELSE so.veroia_switch
                        END THEN stock_vs_charge(
                        CASE
                            WHEN
                            CASE
                                WHEN so.parent_order_id IS NOT NULL THEN sp.direction
                                ELSE so.direction
                            END = 'Export'::text THEN ct_setting('x_export'::text)
                            ELSE ct_setting('x_import'::text)
                        END, svp.pallets)
                        ELSE 0::numeric
                    END + COALESCE(( SELECT sum(h.price) AS sum
                       FROM natord_home h
                      WHERE h.home_nat_load_id = nl.id), 0::numeric)
                    ELSE 0::numeric
                END AS rev_natl
           FROM ct_round_trips rt
             LEFT JOIN ct_rt_legs l ON l.rt_id = rt.id
             LEFT JOIN orders o ON o.id = l.order_id
             LEFT JOIN orders p ON p.id = o.parent_order_id
             LEFT JOIN stock_v_rt_amounts sa ON sa.order_id = o.id
             LEFT JOIN stock_v_pieces vp ON vp.piece_kind = 'intl'::text AND vp.piece_id = o.id
             LEFT JOIN national_loads nl ON nl.id = l.nat_load_id
             LEFT JOIN orders so ON so.id = nl.source_order_id
             LEFT JOIN orders sp ON sp.id = so.parent_order_id
             LEFT JOIN stock_v_pieces svp ON svp.piece_kind = 'intl'::text AND svp.piece_id = so.id
        )
 SELECT rt_id,
    COALESCE(sum(rev_intl + rev_natl), 0::numeric) AS revenue,
    COALESCE(sum(rev_intl), 0::numeric) AS revenue_intl,
    COALESCE(sum(rev_natl), 0::numeric) AS revenue_natl
   FROM legs
  GROUP BY rt_id;
  CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.ct_v_rt_costs AS
 WITH lines AS (
         SELECT ct_cost_lines.rt_id,
            sum(ct_cost_lines.net) FILTER (WHERE ct_cost_lines.category <> 'partner_rate'::text) AS net_other,
            sum(ct_cost_lines.vat) AS vat,
            sum(ct_cost_lines.net) FILTER (WHERE ct_cost_lines.category = 'partner_rate'::text) AS partner_invoiced
           FROM ct_cost_lines
          WHERE ct_cost_lines.rt_id IS NOT NULL
          GROUP BY ct_cost_lines.rt_id
        ), carried AS (
         SELECT l.rt_id,
            o.id AS order_id
           FROM ct_rt_legs l
             JOIN orders o ON o.id = l.order_id
        UNION
         SELECT l.rt_id,
            s.id
           FROM ct_rt_legs l
             JOIN orders o ON o.id = l.order_id AND o.parent_order_id IS NOT NULL
             JOIN orders s ON s.parent_order_id = o.parent_order_id AND s.id <> o.id AND s.deleted_at IS NULL AND s.partner_id IS NOT NULL
          WHERE NOT (EXISTS ( SELECT 1
                   FROM ct_rt_legs l2
                     JOIN ct_round_trips r2 ON r2.id = l2.rt_id
                  WHERE l2.order_id = s.id AND r2.status <> 'cancelled'::text))
        ), planned AS (
         SELECT x.rt_id,
            sum(x.partner_rate) AS partner_planned
           FROM ( SELECT c.rt_id,
                    pa.partner_rate
                   FROM carried c
                     JOIN LATERAL ( SELECT pa_1.partner_rate
                           FROM partner_assignments pa_1
                          WHERE pa_1.order_id = c.order_id AND pa_1.deleted_at IS NULL AND pa_1.status <> 'Cancelled'::text
                          ORDER BY pa_1.id DESC
                         LIMIT 1) pa ON true
                UNION ALL
                 SELECT l.rt_id,
                    nl.partner_rate
                   FROM ct_rt_legs l
                     JOIN national_loads nl ON nl.id = l.nat_load_id
                  WHERE nl.deleted_at IS NULL AND COALESCE(nl.is_partner_trip, false) AND nl.partner_rate IS NOT NULL) x
          GROUP BY x.rt_id
        )
 SELECT rt.id AS rt_id,
    COALESCE(li.net_other, 0::numeric) + COALESCE(NULLIF(li.partner_invoiced, 0::numeric), pl.partner_planned, 0::numeric) + COALESCE(dl.trip_value, 0::numeric) + COALESCE(dl.expenses, 0::numeric) AS lines_net,
    COALESCE(li.vat, 0::numeric) AS vat,
        CASE
            WHEN rt.trip_type = 'OWNED'::text AND rt.total_km IS NOT NULL THEN round(COALESCE(w.eur_per_km, ct_setting('wear_fallback_eur_km'::text)) * rt.total_km::numeric, 2)
            ELSE 0::numeric
        END AS wear,
    dl.trip_value AS dl_trip_value,
    dl.expenses AS dl_expenses,
    dl.id IS NOT NULL AND dl.trip_value IS NULL AS driver_pay_pending,
    rt.trip_type = 'OWNED'::text AND rt.driver_id IS NOT NULL AND dl.id IS NULL AS driver_pay_missing,
    COALESCE(pl.partner_planned, 0::numeric) AS partner_planned,
    COALESCE(li.partner_invoiced, 0::numeric) AS partner_invoiced
   FROM ct_round_trips rt
     LEFT JOIN lines li ON li.rt_id = rt.id
     LEFT JOIN planned pl ON pl.rt_id = rt.id
     LEFT JOIN dl_entries dl ON dl.rt_id = rt.id AND dl.deleted_at IS NULL
     LEFT JOIN ct_v_wear_rate w ON w.truck_id = rt.truck_id;
  DROP VIEW public.ct_v_rt_pnl;
  CREATE VIEW public.ct_v_rt_pnl AS
 SELECT rt.id,
    rt.code,
    rt.scope,
    rt.trip_type,
    rt.truck_id,
    rt.driver_id,
    rt.partner_id,
    rt.date_start,
    rt.date_end,
    rt.status,
    rt.total_km,
    r.revenue,
    c.lines_net + c.wear AS cost_net,
    c.vat AS cost_vat,
    c.lines_net + c.wear + c.vat AS cost_gross,
    r.revenue - (c.lines_net + c.wear + c.vat) AS profit_worst,
    r.revenue - (c.lines_net + c.wear) AS profit_ex_vat,
        CASE
            WHEN r.revenue > 0::numeric THEN round((r.revenue - (c.lines_net + c.wear + c.vat)) / r.revenue * 100::numeric, 1)
            ELSE NULL::numeric
        END AS margin_worst_pct,
        CASE
            WHEN r.revenue > 0::numeric THEN round((r.revenue - (c.lines_net + c.wear)) / r.revenue * 100::numeric, 1)
            ELSE NULL::numeric
        END AS margin_ex_vat_pct,
    c.dl_trip_value,
    c.dl_expenses,
    c.driver_pay_pending,
    c.driver_pay_missing,
    c.partner_planned,
    c.partner_invoiced,
    r.revenue_intl,
    r.revenue_natl
   FROM ct_round_trips rt
     JOIN ct_v_rt_revenue r ON r.rt_id = rt.id
     JOIN ct_v_rt_costs c ON c.rt_id = rt.id
  WHERE rt.status <> 'cancelled'::text;
  REVOKE ALL ON public.ct_v_rt_pnl FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
  FOR v_k IN 1 .. coalesce(array_length(v_grants, 1), 0) LOOP EXECUTE v_grants[v_k]; END LOOP;
  CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.dl_v_rt_route WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
 WITH leg AS (
         SELECT l.rt_id,
            l.id AS leg_id,
            l.direction,
            l.seq,
            COALESCE(o.loading_datetime::timestamp with time zone, nl.loading_datetime)::date AS load_d,
            COALESCE(o.actual_delivery_date, nl.actual_delivery_date, o.delivery_datetime, nl.delivery_datetime::date) AS deliv_d,
            COALESCE(o.loading_location_1_id, nl.pickup_location_1_id) AS from_id,
            COALESCE(o.unloading_location_10_id, o.unloading_location_9_id, o.unloading_location_8_id, o.unloading_location_7_id, o.unloading_location_6_id, o.unloading_location_5_id, o.unloading_location_4_id, o.unloading_location_3_id, o.unloading_location_2_id, o.unloading_location_1_id, nl.delivery_location_10_id, nl.delivery_location_9_id, nl.delivery_location_8_id, nl.delivery_location_7_id, nl.delivery_location_6_id, nl.delivery_location_5_id, nl.delivery_location_4_id, nl.delivery_location_3_id, nl.delivery_location_2_id, nl.delivery_location_1_id) AS to_id,
            ( SELECT count(*) AS count
                   FROM ( VALUES (o.loading_location_2_id), (o.loading_location_3_id), (o.loading_location_4_id), (o.loading_location_5_id), (o.loading_location_6_id), (o.loading_location_7_id), (o.loading_location_8_id), (o.loading_location_9_id), (o.loading_location_10_id), (o.unloading_location_2_id), (o.unloading_location_3_id), (o.unloading_location_4_id), (o.unloading_location_5_id), (o.unloading_location_6_id), (o.unloading_location_7_id), (o.unloading_location_8_id), (o.unloading_location_9_id), (o.unloading_location_10_id), (nl.pickup_location_2_id), (nl.pickup_location_3_id), (nl.pickup_location_4_id), (nl.pickup_location_5_id), (nl.pickup_location_6_id), (nl.pickup_location_7_id), (nl.pickup_location_8_id), (nl.pickup_location_9_id), (nl.pickup_location_10_id), (nl.delivery_location_2_id), (nl.delivery_location_3_id), (nl.delivery_location_4_id), (nl.delivery_location_5_id), (nl.delivery_location_6_id), (nl.delivery_location_7_id), (nl.delivery_location_8_id), (nl.delivery_location_9_id), (nl.delivery_location_10_id)) v(x)
                  WHERE v.x IS NOT NULL) AS extra_stops
           FROM ct_rt_legs l
             LEFT JOIN orders o ON o.id = l.order_id
             LEFT JOIN national_loads nl ON nl.id = l.nat_load_id
        )
 SELECT leg.rt_id,
    string_agg((COALESCE(lf.city, lf.name) || U&' \2192 '::text) || COALESCE(lt.city, lt.name), U&' \00B7 '::text ORDER BY leg.seq, leg.load_d, leg.leg_id) AS route_text,
    jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('dir', leg.direction, 'load', leg.load_d, 'deliv', leg.deliv_d, 'from', jsonb_build_object('name', lf.name, 'city', lf.city, 'country', lf.country), 'to', jsonb_build_object('name', lt.name, 'city', lt.city, 'country', lt.country), 'extra_stops', leg.extra_stops) ORDER BY leg.seq, leg.load_d, leg.leg_id) AS route_legs
   FROM leg
     LEFT JOIN locations lf ON lf.id = leg.from_id
     LEFT JOIN locations lt ON lt.id = leg.to_id
  GROUP BY leg.rt_id;
  CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.ct_v_rt_pallet_gate WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
 WITH rt_orders AS (
         SELECT l.rt_id,
            l.order_id
           FROM ct_rt_legs l
             JOIN orders o ON o.id = l.order_id AND o.deleted_at IS NULL
          WHERE l.order_id IS NOT NULL AND o.pallet_exchange = true
        ), sheets AS (
         SELECT DISTINCT pl_movements.order_id
           FROM pl_movements
          WHERE pl_movements.status = 'confirmed'::text AND (pl_movements.event_type = ANY (ARRAY['PARTNER_PICKUP'::text, 'PARTNER_DROPOFF'::text])) AND pl_movements.order_id IS NOT NULL
        )
 SELECT ro.rt_id,
    count(*) AS legs_needing_sheet,
    count(s.order_id) AS legs_with_sheet,
    count(*) = count(s.order_id) AS sheets_ok
   FROM rt_orders ro
     LEFT JOIN sheets s ON s.order_id = ro.order_id
  GROUP BY ro.rt_id;
  CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.stock_v_pieces AS
 SELECT o.stock_lot_id AS lot_id,
    'intl'::text AS piece_kind,
    o.id AS piece_id,
    o.legacy_id AS piece_rec,
    o.client_id,
    o.loading_location_1_id AS pickup_location_id,
    order_pallets(o.*) AS pallets,
    o.status,
    o.loading_datetime AS loading_date,
    COALESCE(o.status = 'Delivered'::text, false) AS delivered,
        CASE
            WHEN o.status = 'Delivered'::text THEN COALESCE(o.actual_delivery_date, o.delivery_datetime)
            ELSE NULL::date
        END AS delivered_on,
    o.truck_id IS NOT NULL OR o.partner_id IS NOT NULL AS on_truck,
    o.created_at
   FROM orders o
  WHERE o.deleted_at IS NULL AND o.stock_lot_id IS NOT NULL
UNION ALL
 SELECT n.stock_lot_id AS lot_id,
    'natl'::text AS piece_kind,
    n.id AS piece_id,
    n.legacy_id AS piece_rec,
    n.client_id,
    n.pickup_location_1_id AS pickup_location_id,
    COALESCE(n.pallets, 0::numeric) AS pallets,
    n.status,
    n.loading_datetime AS loading_date,
    stock_natl_delivered(n.status, n.delivery_datetime) AS delivered,
        CASE
            WHEN stock_natl_delivered(n.status, n.delivery_datetime) THEN COALESCE(n.actual_delivery_date, n.delivery_datetime)
            ELSE NULL::date
        END AS delivered_on,
    n.truck_id IS NOT NULL OR n.partner_id IS NOT NULL AS on_truck,
    n.created_at
   FROM national_orders n
  WHERE n.deleted_at IS NULL AND n.stock_lot_id IS NOT NULL;
  DROP VIEW public.ct_v_v5_revenue;
  DROP FUNCTION public.plan_h_pallet_sync(bigint[],text,text);
  DROP INDEX public.pl_mov_stop_once;

  -- PROOFS
  EXECUTE snap_sql INTO v_snap1;
  IF v_snap1 <> v_snap0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION '073 rollback proof: a number changed (% -> %)', v_snap0, v_snap1; END IF;
  SELECT relacl::text INTO v_acl1 FROM pg_class WHERE oid = 'public.ct_v_rt_pnl'::regclass;
  IF v_acl1 IS DISTINCT FROM v_acl0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION '073 rollback proof: ct_v_rt_pnl rights % -> %', v_acl0, v_acl1; END IF;
  SELECT count(*) INTO v_k FROM (VALUES
      ('public.ct_v_rt_revenue', '7bd4c2b3206af115a5cfff5478dfdfc9'),
      ('public.ct_v_rt_costs', '3349bcf0e25e567075243f94755de4ff'),
      ('public.ct_v_rt_pnl', 'abc2a49270d8ac7bda9852782ab21f78'),
      ('public.dl_v_rt_route', 'cfb5f85b913657fba8d8d365d38df323'),
      ('public.ct_v_rt_pallet_gate', '3672742c7ac8e537502058c8de6c8e8e'),
      ('public.stock_v_pieces', '9a17994f3476293c585e3ace7a07e2be')) g(rel, m)
   WHERE md5(pg_get_viewdef(to_regclass(g.rel), true)) <> g.m;
  IF v_k <> 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION '073 rollback proof: % view(s) are not the 23/10 text', v_k; END IF;
  IF (SELECT reloptions FROM pg_class WHERE oid = 'public.dl_v_rt_route'::regclass) IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['security_invoker=true']
     OR (SELECT reloptions FROM pg_class WHERE oid = 'public.ct_v_rt_pallet_gate'::regclass) IS DISTINCT FROM ARRAY['security_invoker=true'] THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073 rollback proof: security_invoker lost';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_notify('pgrst', 'reload schema');
  RAISE NOTICE '073 ROLLBACK OK: 5 functions back to 072, 6 views back to 23/10, the money view, the helper and the index gone; numbers %', v_snap1;
END $do$;

SELECT CASE WHEN to_regprocedure('public.plan_h_pallet_sync(bigint[],text,text)') IS NULL AND to_regclass('public.ct_v_v5_revenue') IS NULL
             AND to_regclass('public.pl_mov_stop_once') IS NULL THEN '073 ROLLED BACK' ELSE '073 STILL IN PLACE' END AS result,
       md5(pg_get_viewdef('public.ct_v_rt_revenue'::regclass, true)) AS revenue_md5,
       (SELECT md5(prosrc) FROM pg_proc WHERE oid = 'public.plan_h_leg_add(bigint,bigint,bigint,bigint,text,text,integer)'::regprocedure) AS leg_add_md5;
```

> **Υλοποίηση Task 4 (26/10/2026, κλάδος `feat/national-v5-f3`).** Τα τέσσερα αρχεία ξεκινούν από τα Steps 1–4 κατά γράμμα, με τις εξής αλλαγές:
> 1. Το verify ελέγχει τα md5 που **αποδεικνύει** η 073, όχι τα προσχέδια του κειμένου: PART 5 `ct_v_v5_revenue` `c9dc289a…`, `ct_v_rt_revenue` `ad3c2b61…`· PART 2 `plan_h_drop_consequences` `e370e52c…`, `plan_order_update` `114a6555…`, `plan_rt_update` `b7acd8a0…`. Το `stop_once` απαιτεί και τη στενή D-9 (`LOADING`/`DELIVERY`).
> 2. Το rollback επιστρέφει τις 5 συναρτήσεις στα md5 του PART 0 (072 + 072b: `80b789fc…`, `3ce41ac4…`, ~~`e918ef7c…`~~ **`e427e7a2…`**) και ελέγχει το md5 μετά από κάθε συνάρτηση. Αρνείται όσο υπάρχουν οι έλεγχοι του 073b (B-78..B-90, B-94). Σειρά: 073b → 073 → 072b → 072.
> 3. **Δικαιώματα του `ct_v_rt_pnl`.** Μετρήθηκε στην παραγωγή (SELECT 26/10): οι 5 όψεις έχουν `service_role=arwDxtm/postgres`, και μια νέα όψη του postgres στο `public` παίρνει από τα default privileges `service_role=arwxtm`. Το κείμενο του Step 4 (REVOKE μόνο από PUBLIC/anon/authenticated) περνούσε σήμερα κατά σύμπτωση· με ευρύτερα defaults θα σταματούσε στην απόδειξη acl. Γι' αυτό το rollback αφαιρεί πρώτα **κάθε** δικαίωμα που έδωσε το CREATE και μετά δίνει ακριβώς τα αποθηκευμένα (με `WITH GRANT OPTION` όπου υπήρχε). Το `plan-073.test.mjs` στήνει τα δικαιώματα της παραγωγής και συγκρίνει το acl των 6 όψεων πριν και μετά.
> 4. Το αποτύπωμα κρατά **τις ίδιες** 16 λίστες με το `snap_sql` της 073 (+ μισθοδοσία), όχι υποσύνολο: μία λίστα ανά γεγονός.
> 5. Η κεφαλίδα του dry run λέει «μετά την 072 και την 072b».
>
> Αποτέλεσμα: `plan-073.test.mjs` **8/8**. Ο έλεγχος του Task 5 όπως γράφτηκε θέλει μία προσαρμογή: η 073 σηκώνει και `bad_input` (ο κανόνας μη πεπερασμένων τιμών του Task 2, περιπτώσεις 35–36), όχι μόνο `pallet_fraction`.

### Task 5: Έλεγχοι build χωρίς βάση: `tests/sql-073-drafts.test.js`

**Files:** Create: `tests/sql-073-drafts.test.js`

- [ ] **Step 1: Γράψε το test**

`tests/sql-073-drafts.test.js`:
```js
// tests/sql-073-drafts.test.js - run: node --test tests/sql-073-drafts.test.js
// The 073 drafts (national v5 money, route, pallets, F3) at build time, plain node, no database (principle 6):
//   - the six files and the dry-run builder are there; ASCII only (clipboard has mangled Greek before);
//   - the writing files are ONE DO block; verify and fingerprint write nothing; the dry run is regenerated;
//   - the live views and functions 073 replaces are guarded by md5 BEFORE, and their new texts proved AFTER, with
//     the same lists in 073, verify and rollback (one list per fact, principle 3);
//   - 073 adds no table, column or trigger (B-54 stays); every new object is born closed;
//   - every view that has security_invoker today is rewritten WITH it (CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW drops it otherwise:
//     measured on PGlite 17, 23/10);
//   - every plan: code 073 raises has a case in the rules test; every consequence kind it adds is in the registry;
//   - the money choices M-1..M-3 are marked where the owner's answer goes, so a review finds them.
// The rules are proven by 073_plan_money_test.sql on PGlite (tms-auditor/test/plan-073.test.mjs).
'use strict';
const { test } = require('node:test');
const assert = require('node:assert');
const fs = require('node:fs');
const path = require('node:path');
const { execFileSync } = require('node:child_process');

const ROOT = path.join(__dirname, '..');
const DIR = path.join(ROOT, 'worker/migrations/drafts');   // until the owner runs 073 (then: worker/migrations)
const read = (f) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(DIR, f), 'utf8');
const strip = (s) => s.replace(/--[^\n]*/g, '');
const DO_FILES = ['073_plan_money.sql', '073_plan_money_dryrun.sql', '073_plan_money_test.sql', '073_plan_money_rollback.sql'];
const READ_ONLY = ['073_plan_money_verify.sql', '073_plan_money_fingerprint.sql'];
// The registry of consequence kinds the board (F4) knows: F2's eight + 073's one.
const KINDS = ['invoiced', 'costs', 'payroll_amount', 'local_loading', 'local_payroll', 'rt_cancelled', 'executed_leg',
  'later_leg', 'pallets_confirmed'];

test('073: every file is there and ASCII only', () => {
  for (const f of [...DO_FILES, ...READ_ONLY, 'tools/build-073-dryrun.mjs']) {
    assert.ok(fs.existsSync(path.join(DIR, f)), f);
    const bad = read(f).split('\n').map((l, i) => [i + 1, l]).filter(([, l]) => /[^\x00-\x7f]/.test(l));
    assert.deepStrictEqual(bad, [], `${f}: non-ASCII lines`);
  }
});

test('073: one DO block per writing file; verify and fingerprint only read', () => {
  for (const f of DO_FILES) assert.strictEqual((strip(read(f)).match(/^DO \$/gm) || []).length, 1, f);
  for (const f of READ_ONLY) {
    const s = strip(read(f));
    assert.ok(!/\b(insert|update|delete|create|alter|drop|grant|revoke|truncate)\b/i.test(s.replace(/updated_at|deleted_at|created_at/g, '')), f);
  }
});

test('073: the dry run is the generated one', () => {
  execFileSync(process.execPath, [path.join(DIR, 'tools/build-073-dryrun.mjs'), '--check']);
});

test('073: one md5 list per fact - guards, proofs, verify and rollback agree', () => {
  const src = read('073_plan_money.sql');
  const part0 = src.slice(src.indexOf('===== PART 0'), src.indexOf('===== PART 1'));
  const part5 = src.slice(src.indexOf('===== PART 5'));
  const pairs = (s) => [...s.matchAll(/\('(public\.[a-z0-9_]+(?:\([^)]*\))?)',\s*'([0-9a-f]{32})'\)/g)].map((m) => `${m[1]}=${m[2]}`);
  const before = pairs(part0);
  const hooks = [...src.matchAll(/\('(public\.[a-z_]+\([^)]*\))',\n\s+E'[\s\S]*?,\n\s+E'[\s\S]*?,\n\s+'([0-9a-f]{32})'\)/g)].map((m) => `${m[1]}=${m[2]}`);
  const after = pairs(part5).concat(hooks);
  assert.strictEqual(before.length, 22, 'PART 0 guards 12 views + 10 functions');
  assert.strictEqual(hooks.length, 5, 'five hooks in 072');
  const verify = pairs(read('073_plan_money_verify.sql'));
  assert.deepStrictEqual(verify.sort(), after.sort(), 'verify checks exactly the texts 073 proves');
  const rb = read('073_plan_money_rollback.sql');
  for (const p of before.filter((x) => /ct_v_rt_|dl_v_rt_route|stock_v_pieces/.test(x) && !/stock_v_rt_amounts|wear/.test(x))) {
    assert.ok(rb.includes(`'${p.split('=')[0]}', '${p.split('=')[1]}'`), `rollback proves ${p}`);
  }
  for (const h of hooks) assert.ok(rb.includes(h.split('=')[1]), `rollback reads the 073 text of ${h}`);
});

test('073: no table, no column, no trigger; born closed; security_invoker kept', () => {
  const s = strip(read('073_plan_money.sql'));
  assert.ok(!/create\s+(table|trigger)|alter\s+table|add\s+column|create\s+constraint\s+trigger/i.test(s));
  assert.match(s, /REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION public\.plan_h_pallet_sync\(bigint\[\],text,text\) FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role;/);
  assert.match(s, /REVOKE ALL ON public\.ct_v_v5_revenue FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated, service_role, tms_reader;/);
  assert.match(s, /GRANT SELECT ON public\.ct_v_v5_revenue TO service_role, tms_check_runner;/);
  for (const v of ['dl_v_rt_route', 'ct_v_rt_pallet_gate']) {
    assert.match(s, new RegExp(`CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public\\.${v} WITH \\(security_invoker = true\\) AS`), v);
    assert.match(strip(read('073_plan_money_rollback.sql')), new RegExp(`CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public\\.${v} WITH \\(security_invoker = true\\) AS`), `rollback ${v}`);
  }
  // the helper runs as its caller (072 PART 5: every plan_h_* is invoker)
  const fn = s.slice(s.indexOf('CREATE FUNCTION public.plan_h_pallet_sync'), s.indexOf('$f$;', s.indexOf('CREATE FUNCTION public.plan_h_pallet_sync')));
  assert.ok(!/security\s+definer/i.test(fn) && /SET search_path = public/.test(fn));
});

test('073: every code has a case, every consequence kind is registered, the money choices are marked', () => {
  const src = read('073_plan_money.sql');
  const codes = [...new Set([...src.matchAll(/plan_h_raise\('([a-z_]+)'/g)].map((m) => m[1]))];
  assert.deepStrictEqual(codes, ['pallet_fraction']);
  const t = read('073_plan_money_test.sql');
  for (const c of codes) assert.match(t, new RegExp(`, 'plan:${c}'`), c);
  const kinds = [...src.matchAll(/'kind'', ''([a-z_]+)''/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
  for (const k of kinds) assert.ok(KINDS.includes(k), `consequence kind ${k} not in the registry`);
  for (const m of ['[M-1 WHICH CYCLE]', '[M-2 THE INTERNATIONAL\'S X]', '[M-3 ROUNDING]']) assert.ok(src.includes(m), m);
  assert.ok((t.match(/^ {4}, '(ok|plan:[a-z_]+|con:[a-z_]+)'/gm) || []).length >= 32);
});

test('073: no mirror and no write outside the plan', () => {
  const s = strip(read('073_plan_money.sql')).toLowerCase();
  assert.ok(!/update\s+national_orders/.test(s), 'national_orders is never written (spec 2.1)');
  assert.ok(!/update\s+ct_round_trips/.test(s));
  assert.ok(!/delete\s+from\s+pl_movements/.test(s), 'a pallet row is withdrawn (reversed), never deleted (D-7)');
});
```

-- id: B-78
-- title: Παραγγελία με ζωντανό εθνικό φορτίο του παλιού μοντέλου (NL/GL) ΚΑΙ αποστολή v5
-- flows: F-23,F-14
-- severity: P1
-- schedule: hourly
-- red: > 0
-- baseline:
-- queue: no
-- entity: orders
-- impact: Η ίδια παραγγελία κινείται και στα δύο μοντέλα: το X της VS ή η τιμή της εθνικής θα μετρούσε δύο φορές (Direct/«σπίτι» NL και μερίδια v5). Η 073 το κόβει στην όψη, αλλά η ίδια παραγγελία φαίνεται και στα δύο board.
-- next: Εβδομαδιαίο Εθνικών: ποιο από τα δύο ισχύει; Το NL/GL αποσυνδέεται (074) ή η αποστολή σβήνεται, με «ναι» του owner.
-- exceptions: spec 8 «ίδια παραγγελία με NL και με αποστολή»: VS με ζωντανή αποστολή γέφυρας (shipments.order_id) και ζωντανό Direct NL της ίδιας διεθνούς, και εθνική με ζωντανή αποστολή v5 (shipments.nat_order_id) και ζωντανό NL (source_national_order_id) ή γραμμή groupage. Πριν από το L δεν υπάρχει αποστολή: 0.
-- tolerance: 
-- source: DRAFT 073b (plan F3, spec 8) · PGlite 23/10: 0 on the production copy, red in the scenario of plan-073b.test.mjs
-- enabled: yes
SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM orders o WHERE o.deleted_at IS NULL
 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shipments s WHERE s.order_id = o.id AND s.deleted_at IS NULL)
 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM national_loads nl WHERE nl.source_order_id = o.id AND nl.source_type = 'Direct' AND nl.deleted_at IS NULL))
 + (SELECT count(*) FROM national_orders n WHERE n.deleted_at IS NULL
 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shipments s WHERE s.nat_order_id = n.id AND s.deleted_at IS NULL)
 AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM national_loads nl WHERE nl.source_national_order_id = n.id AND nl.deleted_at IS NULL)
  OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM groupage_lines gl WHERE gl.national_order_id = n.id AND gl.deleted_at IS NULL)));
-- @ids
SELECT coalesce(array_agg(x ORDER BY x), '{}') FROM (SELECT o.legacy_id AS x FROM orders o WHERE o.deleted_at IS NULL
 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shipments s WHERE s.order_id = o.id AND s.deleted_at IS NULL)
 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM national_loads nl WHERE nl.source_order_id = o.id AND nl.source_type = 'Direct' AND nl.deleted_at IS NULL)
 UNION ALL
 SELECT n.legacy_id FROM national_orders n WHERE n.deleted_at IS NULL
 AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shipments s WHERE s.nat_order_id = n.id AND s.deleted_at IS NULL)
 AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM national_loads nl WHERE nl.source_national_order_id = n.id AND nl.deleted_at IS NULL)
  OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM groupage_lines gl WHERE gl.national_order_id = n.id AND gl.deleted_at IS NULL))
 ORDER BY 1 LIMIT 50) s

-- id: B-79

-- flows: F-23,F-36
-- severity: P1
-- schedule: daily
-- red: > 0
-- baseline:
-- queue: no
-- entity: orders

-- next: Owner: ct_v_v5_revenue της παραγγελίας (μερίδια ανά κομμάτι). Αν άλλαξε το X στις ρυθμίσεις, ενημερώνεται και αυτός ο έλεγχος (επιλογή owner M-4).
-- exceptions: [M-4 draft = spec 8 κατά γράμμα] Οι σταθερές 650/850 είναι δεύτερη πηγή του X (αρχή 3) και κομμάτι παρτίδας 057 με VS έχει X x παλέτες/33 (π.χ. 393,94): χτυπά ως ψευδής συναγερμός. Η εναλλακτική του πλάνου συγκρίνει με το X της παραγγελίας από την όψη.

- [ ] **Step 2: Η γεννήτρια του 073b**

`worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073b.mjs`:
```js
// Generates 073b_plan_monitoring.sql (and its dry run) FROM tms-auditor/checks/B-78..B-90.sql:
//   node worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073b.mjs          (writes both)
//   node worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073b.mjs --check  (exit 1 if a committed file drifted)
// Why generated (principle 3): the check files are the ONE source of a check (047b, 066 B-77); the migration that
// puts them into production carries their text byte for byte (Greek as base64) and proves it with md5.
// What 073b does, in ONE DO block: the read policy monitor_read on the five v5 tables (without it the auditor's role
// reads 0 rows from them and every v5 check is green for ever - RLS on, no policy, 071), and the 13 checks of spec 8
// that do not need day L. ASCII in, ASCII out.
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { loadChecks } from '../../../../tms-auditor/checks/load.mjs';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
export const DIR = path.resolve(HERE, '..');
export const IDS = ['B-78', 'B-79', 'B-80', 'B-81', 'B-82', 'B-83', 'B-84', 'B-85', 'B-86', 'B-87', 'B-88', 'B-89', 'B-90'];
const V5 = ['shipments', 'shipment_pieces', 'rt_stops', 'piece_legs', 'plan_notices'];
const md5 = (s) => crypto.createHash('md5').update(s, 'utf8').digest('hex');
const b64 = (s) => Buffer.from(s, 'utf8').toString('base64');
const txt = (s) => (s == null ? 'NULL' : /^[\x20-\x7e\n]*$/.test(s) && !s.includes('$m$') ? `$m$${s}$m$`
  : `convert_from(decode('${b64(s)}', 'base64'), 'UTF8')`);

export function build() {
  const { checks, errors } = loadChecks();
  if (errors.length) throw new Error('catalog lint: ' + errors.join('; '));
  const mine = IDS.map((id) => checks.find((c) => c.id === id));
  if (mine.some((c) => !c)) throw new Error('missing check file: ' + IDS.filter((id, i) => !mine[i]).join(','));
  const rows = mine.map((c) => `    (${[txt(c.id), txt(c.title), `ARRAY[${c.flows.map(txt).join(',')}]::text[]`, txt(c.sql), txt(c.ids_sql),
    txt(c.entity_table), txt(c.red_op), c.red_value, c.baseline ?? 'NULL', txt(c.severity), txt(c.schedule), c.is_queue,
    txt(c.impact), txt(c.next_step), txt(c.exceptions), txt(c.tolerance), c.enabled, txt(c.disabled_reason)].join(',\n     ')})`);
  const proof = mine.map((c) => `      ('${c.id}', '${md5(c.sql)}', '${md5(c.ids_sql || '')}')`).join(',\n');
  const body = `DECLARE
  v_k int; v_trg0 int; v_g record; v_v numeric; v_sql text;
BEGIN
  PERFORM set_config('lock_timeout', '5s', true);
  -- 0. GUARDS
  IF to_regclass('public.ct_v_v5_revenue') IS NULL OR to_regprocedure('public.plan_h_pallet_sync(bigint[],text,text)') IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073b: 073 must be applied first';
  END IF;
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM monitoring.checks WHERE id IN (${IDS.map((i) => `'${i}'`).join(', ')})) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073b: already applied (a check B-78..B-90 exists) - run 073b_plan_monitoring_verify instead';
  END IF;
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename IN (${V5.map((t) => `'${t}'`).join(', ')})) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073b: a policy already exists on a v5 table - stop, re-measure';
  END IF;
  SELECT count(*) INTO v_trg0 FROM pg_trigger t JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = t.tgrelid
    JOIN pg_namespace ns ON ns.oid = c.relnamespace WHERE NOT t.tgisinternal AND t.tgenabled <> 'D' AND ns.nspname = 'public';

  -- 1. THE AUDITOR READS THE v5 TABLES (D-14). 071 left them RLS on, no policy: the grants to tms_check_runner and
  --    tms_reader give 0 rows, so a v5 check would be green for ever (principle 1). The same read-only policy as
  --    every table the auditor reads (050, 057's stock_lots); service_role bypasses RLS.
${V5.map((t) => `  CREATE POLICY monitor_read ON public.${t} FOR SELECT TO tms_check_runner, tms_reader USING (true);`).join('\n')}

  -- 1b. X for the auditor (D-15): ct_v_v5_revenue runs as its owner, but ct_setting() and stock_vs_charge() inside it
  --     run as the CALLER (a view does not lend its rights to the functions it calls): without this, B-79 / B-80
  --     fail with "permission denied for table ct_settings" as soon as one v5 row exists (measured on PGlite 23/10).
  --     The auditor's runner only (it already reads money: stock_v_lot_money, 057); never tms_reader.
  GRANT SELECT ON public.ct_settings TO tms_check_runner;
  -- SUPERSEDED (23/10 sketch): build-073b.mjs is the source of 073b. Since 26/10 (PR #105) it also gives the runner
  -- EXECUTE on stock_vs_charge and a read policy on ct_settings (RLS on in production).

  -- 2. THE 13 CHECKS (text = tms-auditor/checks/<id>.sql, generated; md5-proven below)
  INSERT INTO monitoring.checks (id, title, flows, sql_text, ids_sql, entity_table, red_op, red_value, baseline, severity,
    schedule_tag, is_queue, impact, next_step, exceptions, tolerance, enabled, disabled_reason) VALUES
${rows.join(',\n')};
  GET DIAGNOSTICS v_k = ROW_COUNT;
  IF v_k <> ${IDS.length} THEN RAISE EXCEPTION '073b: % checks inserted, expected ${IDS.length}', v_k; END IF;

  -- 3. PROOFS: the text is the file's; each check runs as the auditor's role and says 0 today (no v5 row yet)
  FOR v_g IN SELECT * FROM (VALUES
${proof}
    ) p(id, m_sql, m_ids) LOOP
    IF (SELECT md5(sql_text) <> v_g.m_sql OR md5(coalesce(ids_sql, '')) <> v_g.m_ids FROM monitoring.checks WHERE id = v_g.id) THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION '073b proof: % is not the text of its file', v_g.id;
    END IF;
    SELECT sql_text INTO v_sql FROM monitoring.checks WHERE id = v_g.id;
    SET LOCAL ROLE tms_check_runner;
    EXECUTE v_sql INTO v_v;
    RESET ROLE;
    IF v_v IS DISTINCT FROM 0 THEN RAISE EXCEPTION '073b proof: % gives % today, expected 0 before day L', v_g.id, v_v; END IF;
  END LOOP;
  IF NOT has_table_privilege('tms_check_runner', 'public.ct_settings', 'SELECT')
     OR has_table_privilege('tms_reader', 'public.ct_settings', 'SELECT') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073b proof: ct_settings is not readable by the runner only';
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND policyname = 'monitor_read'
        AND tablename IN (${V5.map((t) => `'${t}'`).join(', ')}) AND cmd = 'SELECT') <> ${V5.length} THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073b proof: the ${V5.length} read policies are not there';
  END IF;
  IF (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_trigger t JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = t.tgrelid JOIN pg_namespace ns ON ns.oid = c.relnamespace
       WHERE NOT t.tgisinternal AND t.tgenabled <> 'D' AND ns.nspname = 'public') <> v_trg0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073b proof: a trigger appeared';
  END IF;
  RAISE NOTICE '073b OK: ${IDS.length} checks, ${V5.length} read policies, X readable by the runner, every check 0 today';
`;
  const head = `-- 073b - NATIONAL v5, PHASE F3: THE AUDITOR LEARNS v5 (spec 8). GENERATED by worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073b.mjs
-- from tms-auditor/checks/${IDS[0]}..${IDS[IDS.length - 1]}.sql. DO NOT EDIT BY HAND (tests/sql-073-drafts.test.js fails on drift).
-- SEPARATE approval from 073 (C-17): run it AFTER 073 and its verify, after 15:00, with an explicit yes:
--   1. 073b_plan_monitoring_dryrun.sql   (this block + a deliberate error: NOTHING kept)
--   2. 073b_plan_monitoring.sql          (this file)
-- ASCII only (Greek texts are base64). ONE DO block.
-- After it: 071_plan_schema_verify.sql shows policies = 5 (expected from 073b on; it was 0 by design of 071).
-- REVERSE: 073b_plan_monitoring_rollback.sql.
-- EXPECTED: NOTICE "073b OK: ${IDS.length} checks, ${V5.length} read policies, X readable by the runner, every check 0 today".
`;
  const main = `${head}\nDO $do$\n${body}END $do$;\n`;
  const dry = `-- 073b DRY RUN - GENERATED by worker/migrations/drafts/tools/build-073b.mjs. DO NOT EDIT BY HAND.
-- 073b's block verbatim, ending with a deliberate error: NOTHING is kept. ASCII. ONE DO block.
-- EXPECTED: NOTICE "073b OK: ..." then ERROR: P0001: DRY RUN 073b finished - EVERYTHING UNDONE, nothing kept.

DO $dry$
${body}  RAISE EXCEPTION 'DRY RUN 073b finished - EVERYTHING UNDONE, nothing kept.';
END
$dry$;
`;
  const rollback = `-- 073b ROLLBACK - the 13 checks out of the catalog (with their results) and the 5 read policies off. ASCII. ONE DO block.
-- After it the auditor reads 0 rows from the v5 tables again (071's design) and no ct_settings; only with an explicit yes, after 15:00.
DO $do$
BEGIN
  PERFORM set_config('lock_timeout', '5s', true);
  DELETE FROM monitoring.results WHERE check_id IN (${IDS.map((i) => `'${i}'`).join(', ')});
  DELETE FROM monitoring.checks WHERE id IN (${IDS.map((i) => `'${i}'`).join(', ')});
${V5.map((t) => `  DROP POLICY IF EXISTS monitor_read ON public.${t};`).join('\n')}
  REVOKE SELECT ON public.ct_settings FROM tms_check_runner;
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_policies WHERE schemaname = 'public' AND tablename IN (${V5.map((t) => `'${t}'`).join(', ')})) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION '073b rollback proof: a policy is left on a v5 table';
  END IF;
  RAISE NOTICE '073b ROLLBACK OK';
END $do$;
`;
  return { main, dry, rollback };
}

if (process.argv[1] && path.resolve(process.argv[1]) === fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)) {
  const out = build();
  const files = { '073b_plan_monitoring.sql': out.main, '073b_plan_monitoring_dryrun.sql': out.dry, '073b_plan_monitoring_rollback.sql': out.rollback };
  if (process.argv.includes('--check')) {
    const drift = Object.entries(files).filter(([f, s]) => !fs.existsSync(path.join(DIR, f)) || fs.readFileSync(path.join(DIR, f), 'utf8') !== s);
    if (drift.length) { console.error('drift: ' + drift.map(([f]) => f).join(', ')); process.exit(1); }
    process.exit(0);
  }
  for (const [f, s] of Object.entries(files)) fs.writeFileSync(path.join(DIR, f), s);
  console.log('wrote 073b: ' + Object.keys(files).join(', '));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Το test του 073b**

`tms-auditor/test/plan-073b.test.mjs`:
```js
// 073b (the auditor learns v5, spec 8) on the production-shaped PGlite copy of plan-073.test.mjs: every new check is
// 0 on today's data (no false alarm before day L) and RED in a scenario that breaks its rule; without the read
// policies the auditor's role sees no v5 row, so the same scenario stays green (why D-14 exists).
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { freshDb } from './helpers.mjs';
import { DRAFTS } from '../lib/db.mjs';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const read = (f) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(DRAFTS, f), 'utf8');
const readDone = (f) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(DRAFTS, '..', f), 'utf8');
// 072: executed by the owner before 073 (F2 Task K3 moves it out of drafts/); a checkout before that still has it there
const read072 = () => (fs.existsSync(path.join(DRAFTS, '072_plan_functions.sql')) ? fs.readFileSync(path.join(DRAFTS, '072_plan_functions.sql'), 'utf8') : readDone('072_plan_functions.sql'));
const fixture = (f) => fs.readFileSync(path.join(HERE, 'fixtures', f), 'utf8');
const { btree_gist: btreeGist } = await import('@electric-sql/pglite/contrib/btree_gist');
const { pgcrypto } = await import('@electric-sql/pglite/contrib/pgcrypto');
const NEW = ['B-78', 'B-79', 'B-80', 'B-81', 'B-82', 'B-83', 'B-84', 'B-85', 'B-86', 'B-87', 'B-88', 'B-89', 'B-90'];

async function prodShaped() {
  const db = await freshDb({ v5: false, extensions: { btree_gist: btreeGist, pgcrypto } });
  await db.exec(`DO $r$ BEGIN
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'anon') THEN CREATE ROLE anon NOLOGIN; END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'authenticated') THEN CREATE ROLE authenticated NOLOGIN; END IF;
    IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_roles WHERE rolname = 'service_role') THEN CREATE ROLE service_role NOLOGIN; END IF;
  END $r$;`);
  for (const f of ['live-071.sql', 'live-072.sql', 'live-073.sql']) await db.exec(fixture(f));
  await db.exec(`CREATE SCHEMA IF NOT EXISTS extensions; CREATE EXTENSION IF NOT EXISTS pgcrypto WITH SCHEMA extensions;
    INSERT INTO public.locations (legacy_id, name) VALUES ('recJucKOhC1zh4IP3', 'CROSS-DOCK');
    UPDATE monitoring.checks SET red_value = (SELECT count(*) FROM pg_trigger t JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = t.tgrelid
      JOIN pg_namespace ns ON ns.oid = c.relnamespace WHERE NOT t.tgisinternal AND t.tgenabled <> 'D' AND ns.nspname = 'public'),
      baseline = NULL WHERE id = 'B-54';`);
  await db.exec(readDone('071_plan_schema.sql'));
  await db.exec(readDone('071b_plan_one_vehicle.sql'));
  await db.exec(read072());
  await db.exec(fixture('v1-money-073.sql'));
  await db.exec(read('073_plan_money.sql'));
  // the seed of the local catalog already holds the 13 (047b is generated from the files): take them out, as production
  await db.exec(`DELETE FROM monitoring.checks WHERE id IN (${NEW.map((i) => `'${i}'`).join(',')})`);
  return db;
}
async function errorOf(db, sql) { try { await db.exec(sql); return null; } catch (e) { return String(e.message); } }
async function values(db) {   // each new check as the auditor's role, as run_checks does
  const out = {};
  for (const id of NEW) {
    const { sql_text: sql } = (await db.query(`SELECT sql_text FROM monitoring.checks WHERE id = $1`, [id])).rows[0];
    await db.exec('SET ROLE tms_check_runner');
    try { out[id] = Number(Object.values((await db.query(sql)).rows[0])[0]); } finally { await db.exec('RESET ROLE'); }
  }
  return out;
}
// One scenario that breaks every rule once (written with the plan_* where a rule allows it, directly where only a
// bypass can break it - that is what the check is for).
const SCENARIO = `DO $s$
DECLARE td date := (now() AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens')::date; d date; hub bigint; la bigint; lb bigint; cl bigint; pa bigint; trk bigint; trk2 bigint;
  r jsonb; rt bigint; rtp bigint; n bigint; sh bigint; pc bigint; lg bigint; vso bigint; vsb bigint; vsh bigint; vpc bigint; past bigint;
  f timestamptz; t timestamptz;
BEGIN
  d := td + 40; f := (d + time '06:00') AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens'; t := (d + time '08:00') AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens';
  SELECT id INTO hub FROM locations WHERE is_hub;
  INSERT INTO locations (legacy_id, name) VALUES ('rec073bA', 'S073b A') RETURNING id INTO la;
  INSERT INTO locations (legacy_id, name) VALUES ('rec073bB', 'S073b B') RETURNING id INTO lb;
  INSERT INTO clients (company_name) VALUES ('S073b client') RETURNING id INTO cl;
  INSERT INTO partners (company_name) VALUES ('S073b partner') RETURNING id INTO pa;
  INSERT INTO trucks (license_plate) VALUES ('S073B-1') RETURNING id INTO trk;
  INSERT INTO trucks (license_plate) VALUES ('S073B-2') RETURNING id INTO trk2;
  -- B-82 an empty cycle (plan_rt_create alone, never through a plan_* that cancels it) ; B-83 a partner without a rate
  r := plan_rt_create(jsonb_build_object('new_rt', jsonb_build_object('trip_type', 'OWNED', 'truck_id', trk),
         'stops', jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('ref', 'a', 'location_id', la, 'side', 'KATHODOS', 'planned_from', f, 'planned_to', t))), 's073b', 'owner');
  r := plan_rt_create(jsonb_build_object('new_rt', jsonb_build_object('trip_type', 'PARTNER', 'partner_id', pa),
         'stops', jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('ref', 'a', 'location_id', la, 'side', 'KATHODOS', 'planned_from', f, 'planned_to', t))), 's073b', 'owner');
  rtp := (r->>'rt_id')::bigint;
  PERFORM plan_h_finish();
  -- B-84 a cost line left on a cancelled trip
  INSERT INTO ct_cost_lines (rt_id, category, net, vat, alloc_status, pay_source, line_date, created_by)
  SELECT id, 'fuel', 50, 12, 'allocated', 'DKV', td, 's073b' FROM ct_round_trips WHERE code = 'RT-T734';
  -- B-81 + B-90: a national whose day passed and still waits, with its price wiped later (a facade PATCH)
  r := plan_orders_create(jsonb_build_object('orders', jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('client_id', cl, 'price', 100,
         'reference', 'S073b', 'temperature_c', 2, 'pallet_exchange', true, 'shipments', jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object(
         'origin_location_id', la, 'dest_location_id', lb, 'pallets', 5, 'load_from', f - interval '50 days', 'load_to', t - interval '50 days',
         'deliver_from', f - interval '49 days', 'deliver_to', t - interval '49 days'))))), 's073b', 'owner');
  n := (r->'ids'->'nat_order_ids'->>0)::bigint;
  UPDATE national_orders SET price = 0 WHERE id = n;
  -- B-78 (national half): the same national order also has a load of today's model
  INSERT INTO national_loads (legacy_id, source_type, source_national_order_id, status) VALUES ('rec073bNL2', 'National', n, 'Pending');
  -- B-85 two temperatures 2 and 6 on one side ; B-88 the pending row of a leg that went around the plan
  r := plan_orders_create(jsonb_build_object('orders', jsonb_build_array(
         jsonb_build_object('client_id', cl, 'price', 50, 'reference', 'S073b', 'temperature_c', 2, 'pallet_exchange', true,
           'shipments', jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('origin_location_id', la, 'dest_location_id', lb, 'pallets', 3,
             'load_from', f, 'load_to', t, 'deliver_from', f + interval '4 hours', 'deliver_to', t + interval '4 hours'))),
         jsonb_build_object('client_id', cl, 'price', 50, 'reference', 'S073b', 'temperature_c', 6,
           'shipments', jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('origin_location_id', la, 'dest_location_id', lb, 'pallets', 3,
             'load_from', f, 'load_to', t, 'deliver_from', f + interval '4 hours', 'deliver_to', t + interval '4 hours'))),
         jsonb_build_object('client_id', cl, 'price', 50, 'reference', 'S073b', 'temperature_c', 2, 'pallet_exchange', true,
           'shipments', jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('origin_location_id', la, 'dest_location_id', lb, 'pallets', 2,
             'load_from', f, 'load_to', t, 'deliver_from', f + interval '4 hours', 'deliver_to', t + interval '4 hours')))),
         'target', jsonb_build_object('new_rt', jsonb_build_object('trip_type', 'OWNED', 'truck_id', trk2), 'side', 'KATHODOS'),
         'stops', jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('ref', 'a', 'location_id', la, 'side', 'KATHODOS', 'planned_from', f, 'planned_to', t),
                                    jsonb_build_object('ref', 'b', 'location_id', lb, 'side', 'KATHODOS', 'planned_from', f + interval '4 hours', 'planned_to', t + interval '4 hours'))),
         's073b', 'owner');
  lg := (r->'ids'->'leg_ids'->>2)::bigint;   -- the third order's leg: the other two keep the mix of B-85
  UPDATE piece_legs SET deleted_at = now(), deleted_reason = 'bypass', deleted_by = 's073b' WHERE id = lg;
  -- B-86 a notice nobody read for 25 hours
  INSERT INTO plan_notices (to_desk, kind, message, created_by, created_at) VALUES ('natl', 'costs_without_rt', 'S073b', 's073b', now() - interval '25 hours');
  -- B-78 + B-89: a VS import with a bridge AND a Direct NL, and a v5 pallet row on its bridge leg
  SET CONSTRAINTS ALL DEFERRED;   -- plan_h_finish above left them IMMEDIATE for this transaction
  INSERT INTO orders (legacy_id, direction, status, veroia_switch, client_id, price, cross_dock_date, pallet_exchange)
  VALUES ('rec073bO1', 'Import', 'Pending', true, cl, 2000, d, true) RETURNING id INTO vso;
  INSERT INTO order_stops (legacy_id, stop_type, stop_number, datetime, order_id, location_id, pallets) VALUES ('rec073bS1', 'Unloading', 1, d, vso, lb, 10) RETURNING id INTO vsb;
  INSERT INTO shipments (order_id, order_stop_id, created_by) VALUES (vso, vsb, 's073b') RETURNING id INTO vsh;
  INSERT INTO shipment_pieces (shipment_id, pallets, created_by) VALUES (vsh, 10, 's073b') RETURNING id INTO vpc;
  INSERT INTO national_loads (legacy_id, source_type, source_order_id, status) VALUES ('rec073bNL', 'Direct', vso, 'Pending');
END $s$;`;
const SCENARIO2 = `DO $s$
DECLARE hub bigint; lb bigint; vpc bigint; r jsonb; lg bigint; cl bigint; d date := (now() AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens')::date + 40;
  pd date := (now() AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens')::date - 3; tr bigint;
BEGIN
  SELECT id INTO hub FROM locations WHERE is_hub;
  SELECT id INTO lb FROM locations WHERE legacy_id = 'rec073bB';
  SELECT id INTO cl FROM clients WHERE company_name = 'S073b client';
  SELECT p.id INTO vpc FROM shipment_pieces p JOIN shipments s ON s.id = p.shipment_id JOIN orders o ON o.id = s.order_id WHERE o.legacy_id = 'rec073bO1';
  INSERT INTO trucks (license_plate) VALUES ('S073B-3') RETURNING id INTO tr;
  r := plan_put_pieces(jsonb_build_object('pieces', jsonb_build_array(jsonb_build_object('piece_id', vpc)),
         'target', jsonb_build_object('new_rt', jsonb_build_object('trip_type', 'OWNED', 'truck_id', tr), 'side', 'KATHODOS'),
         'stops', jsonb_build_array(
           jsonb_build_object('ref', 'h', 'location_id', hub, 'side', 'KATHODOS', 'planned_from', (pd + time '06:00') AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens', 'planned_to', (pd + time '07:00') AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens'),
           jsonb_build_object('ref', 'b', 'location_id', lb, 'side', 'KATHODOS', 'planned_from', (pd + time '10:00') AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens', 'planned_to', (pd + time '11:00') AT TIME ZONE 'Europe/Athens'))),
         's073b', 'owner');
  lg := (r->'ids'->'leg_ids'->>0)::bigint;
  -- B-89: a second writer on the VS stop (only a bypass can do it: the feeder never writes for a bridge)
  INSERT INTO pl_movements (movement_date, counterparty_type, client_id, location_id, event_type, taken, given, created_by, piece_leg_id)
  VALUES (d, 'CLIENT', cl, lb, 'DELIVERY', 10, 10, 's073b', lg);
  -- B-87: that cycle ended 3 days ago (OWNED, no km)
END $s$;`;

test('073b on the production-shaped copy', async (t) => {
  const db = await prodShaped();

  await t.test('dry run keeps nothing; 073b applies; every new check is 0 today', async () => {
    assert.match(await errorOf(db, read('073b_plan_monitoring_dryrun.sql')) ?? '', /DRY RUN 073b finished - EVERYTHING UNDONE/);
    assert.strictEqual(Number((await db.query(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM pg_policies WHERE policyname = 'monitor_read' AND tablename = 'shipments'`)).rows[0].n), 0);
    assert.strictEqual(await errorOf(db, read('073b_plan_monitoring.sql')), null);
    const v = await values(db);
    assert.deepStrictEqual(Object.values(v).filter((x) => x !== 0), [], JSON.stringify(v));
    for (const tag of ['hourly', 'daily']) await db.query('SELECT monitoring.run_checks($1)', [tag]);
    assert.deepStrictEqual((await db.query(`SELECT check_id FROM monitoring.results WHERE status = 'error'`)).rows, []);
  });

  await t.test('the scenario turns every new check red (X changed in the settings for B-79)', async () => {
    assert.strictEqual(await errorOf(db, SCENARIO), null);
    assert.strictEqual(await errorOf(db, SCENARIO2), null);
    await db.exec(`UPDATE ct_settings SET value = 700 WHERE key = 'x_import'`);
    const v = await values(db);
    const green = Object.entries(v).filter(([id, x]) => x === 0 && id !== 'B-80').map(([id]) => id);
    assert.deepStrictEqual(green, [], JSON.stringify(v));
    assert.strictEqual(v['B-80'], 0, 'B-80 stays 0: the shares add up to the price by construction (M-3 draft)');
    assert.strictEqual(v['B-78'], 2, 'B-78 names the VS with a Direct NL AND the national order with a load of today');
  });

  await t.test('without the read policies the auditor sees no v5 row: silent checks and a false alarm', async () => {
    for (const tb of ['shipments', 'shipment_pieces', 'rt_stops', 'piece_legs', 'plan_notices']) await db.exec(`DROP POLICY monitor_read ON public.${tb}`);
    const v = await values(db);
    for (const id of ['B-85', 'B-86', 'B-88', 'B-89']) assert.strictEqual(v[id], 0, `${id} silent without the policy`);
    const live = Number((await db.query(`SELECT count(*) AS n FROM ct_round_trips WHERE model = 2 AND status <> 'cancelled'`)).rows[0].n);
    assert.strictEqual(v['B-82'], live, 'B-82 counts EVERY v5 cycle as empty: it cannot see a single leg');
  });
});
```

test('073b is the generated one, ASCII, and holds exactly the 13 check files', () => {
  execFileSync(process.execPath, [path.join(DIR, 'tools/build-073b.mjs'), '--check']);
  for (const f of ['073b_plan_monitoring.sql', '073b_plan_monitoring_dryrun.sql', '073b_plan_monitoring_rollback.sql']) {
    assert.ok(!/[^\x00-\x7f]/.test(read(f)), `${f}: non-ASCII`);
  }
  const ids = [...read('073b_plan_monitoring.sql').matchAll(/\('(B-\d+)', '[0-9a-f]{32}', '[0-9a-f]{32}'\)/g)].map((m) => m[1]);
  assert.deepStrictEqual(ids, ['B-78', 'B-79', 'B-80', 'B-81', 'B-82', 'B-83', 'B-84', 'B-85', 'B-86', 'B-87', 'B-88', 'B-89', 'B-90']);
  assert.match(read('073b_plan_monitoring.sql'), /GRANT SELECT ON public\.ct_settings TO tms_check_runner;/);
  assert.ok(!/tms_reader;/.test(read('073b_plan_monitoring.sql').replace(/TO tms_check_runner, tms_reader USING/g, '')), 'no grant to tms_reader');
});

> **Υλοποίηση Task 6 (26/10/2026, κλάδος `feat/national-v5-f3`).** Τα 13 αρχεία ξεκινούν από το Step 1 κατά γράμμα, με τις απαντήσεις του owner (24/10): **B-79 = M-4 β** (`|Σ μεριδίων − total| > 0,01`, και στο `-- @ids`· το `total` της όψης είναι το X της παραγγελίας), **B-80 = M-5 δ** (`-- enabled: no: ταυτολογία ως το L (owner 24/10, M-5 δ) …`), **B-90 = M-6 α** (μένει). Αποκλίσεις από το κείμενο του πλάνου, με το γιατί:
> 1. **Π3-6 της παράδοσης 072b** (δεν είχε γραφτεί εδώ στον Task 0): η 073b **μεταφέρει** τα B-91..B-93 από το `tms-auditor/checks-072b/` στο `tms-auditor/checks/` (`git mv`, ο φάκελος σβήστηκε) και τα σπέρνει μαζί με τα 13. Στο `build-073b.mjs`: `F3_IDS` (13), `HANDOVER_IDS` (3), `IDS` = 16. Το `rt_vehicle_segments` **δεν** μπαίνει στη λίστα πολιτικών (έχει τη δική του από την 072b, H-10)· νέος φρουρός: 072b με την πολιτική της πριν από την 073b. Άλλαξαν μαζί: `plan-072b.test.mjs` (διαβάζει από το `checks/`), `tests/sql-072b-drafts.test.js` (το test «όχι στον κατάλογο ως την 073b» γίνεται «στον κατάλογο από την 073b, `checks-072b/` δεν υπάρχει»), το fixture `schema-v5.sql` (και `rt_vehicle_segments`), τα grants της 072b στο `createDb` και στο `catalog.test` (050 / A1).
> 2. **Μετρήσεις:** κατάλογος **107** έλεγχοι (100 ενεργοί), όχι 104/98· seed `107 checks (100 enabled)`· `schema.sql` χωρίς αλλαγή.
> 3. **`lib/db.mjs`:** το `draftGrants` διαβάζει από το `worker/migrations/`, άρα `V5_SCHEMA = '071_plan_schema.sql'`, `V5_SEGMENTS = 'drafts/072b_plan_vehicle_segments.sql'`, `V5_MONEY = 'drafts/073_plan_money.sql'` και μία λίστα `OWN_GRANTS` (057, 071, 072b, 073) για το `createDb` και το `catalog.test`. Το `{ v5: false }` μπήκε στα `plan-071`, `plan-072` και στο `prod-shaped-072.mjs` (ο ένας κατασκευαστής των 072b/073/073b).
> 4. **Το 073b** (γεννήτρια): ο έλεγχος md5 κρατά και το `enabled` κάθε ελέγχου (ο B-80 φτάνει στην παραγωγή απενεργοποιημένος, αποδεδειγμένα)· κάθε έλεγχος περνά το `monitoring.check_sql_guard` πριν τρέξει ως `tms_check_runner` (ο ίδιος δρόμος με το `run_checks`)· η απόδειξη των πολιτικών ελέγχει ρόλους (ακριβώς οι δύο), `qual = true`, κανένα `with_check`· φρουρός «ο runner διαβάζει ήδη `ct_settings`»· το rollback αποδεικνύει ότι έφυγαν έλεγχοι, πολιτικές και grant· αριθμοί της κεφαλίδας μόνο πεπερασμένοι (κανόνας NaN/Infinity).
> 5. **`plan-073b.test.mjs`:** χτίζεται με τον ίδιο κατασκευαστή με το `plan-073` (`prodShaped072(true, { money: 'live', with072b: true })` + `v1-money-073.sql` + 073). Το σενάριο στέλνει `request_id` (απόφαση 44) και η VS έχει δύο κομμάτια 6 + 4. Με M-4 β ο B-79 **μένει 0** όταν αλλάζει το X στις ρυθμίσεις (το test το ελέγχει) και κοκκινίζει σε **χαλασμένο κανόνα μεριδίων** (λάθος παρονομαστής στην `ct_v_v5_revenue`, μέσα σε ROLLBACK)· ο B-80 μετρά ως απενεργοποιημένος (το `run_checks` δεν τον τρέχει)· οι B-91..B-93 μένουν 0 στο σενάριο. Προστέθηκαν: οι φρουροί (χωρίς 073, χωρίς την πολιτική της 072b), «ήδη εφαρμοσμένη», το rollback της 073 αρνείται όσο υπάρχουν οι έλεγχοι της 073b, `071_plan_schema_verify` `policies = 5` και verify της 072b `auditor_policy = 1` μετά, rollback της 073b. Το `plan-073.test.mjs` βγάζει τα 16 από τον τοπικό κατάλογο, όπως η παραγωγή πριν την 073b.
> 6. **`tests/sql-073-drafts.test.js`:** 9 tests (όχι 8): το test του 073b (λίστα από το `build-073b.mjs`, όχι γραμμένη δεύτερη φορά) και χωριστό test ότι η λίστα άρνησης του `073_plan_money_rollback.sql` = `F3_IDS` (έλεγχος Task 4, ήσσον 2).
> 7. **Ερωτήσεις owner** (στο scratch `owner-questions.md`, αναστρέψιμος δρόμος): Π3-4/Π3-5 (B-83 ανά τμήμα· 💶 αναπάντητο): ο B-83 μένει ανά δρομολόγιο, ίδιος κανόνας με το `partner_rate_missing` της 073· ο B-79 με M-4 β είναι κι αυτός δίχτυ κανόνα (όπως ο B-80) και μένει ενεργός, όπως διάλεξε ο owner.
