- [ ] **Step 2: Το test που αποτυγχάνει**

`worker/test/dkv-match-cycles.test.mjs`:
```js
// 073 / E10 (spec 5): the DKV import matches a v5 cycle (model 2) by the HOUR of the line, inside its window
// start_at..end_at on the Athens clock; two cycles it may belong to are a review (none + the candidates), never a
// silent pick (tech F3). Today's round trips (model 1) are matched EXACTLY as before: the first test freezes
// today's answers (computed from import-rules.mjs @ 9f98208d, 23/10) for a table of lines.
// Synthetic data only: fake plates, trucks and ids (public repo).
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { matchRoundTrip } from '../src/import-rules.mjs';

const RTS = [
  { id: 1, truck_id: 7, status: 'planned', date_start: '2026-10-12', date_end: '2026-10-14' },
  { id: 2, truck_id: 7, status: 'closed', date_start: '2026-10-14', date_end: '2026-10-17' },
  { id: 3, truck_id: 8, status: 'cancelled', date_start: '2026-10-14', date_end: '2026-10-15' },
  { id: 4, truck_id: 8, status: 'planned', date_start: '2026-10-20', date_end: null },
];
const L = [
  { plate: 'XX1', truck_id: 7, service_date: '2026-10-12' },
  { plate: 'XX1', truck_id: 7, service_date: '2026-10-14', time: '10:00' },
  { plate: 'XX1', truck_id: 7, service_date: '2026-10-11' },
  { plate: 'XX1', truck_id: 7, service_date: '2026-10-19' },
  { plate: 'XX1', truck_id: 7, period_from: '2026-10-01', period_to: '2026-10-15' },
  { plate: 'XX2', truck_id: 8, service_date: '2026-10-14' },
  { plate: 'XX2', truck_id: 8, service_date: '2026-10-21', time: '23:00' },
  { plate: null, truck_id: null, service_date: '2026-10-14' },
  { plate: 'ZZ', truck_id: null, service_date: '2026-10-14' },
  { category: 'dkv', plate: 'XX1', truck_id: 7, service_date: '2026-10-14' },
];
const RULES = [{ kind: 'rt_pref', key: 'XX9', value: { rt_id: 2 } }];
const TODAY = [{"match":"sure","rt_id":1,"alternatives":[],"none_reason":null},{"match":"suggest","rt_id":1,"alternatives":[2],"none_reason":null},{"match":"sure","rt_id":1,"alternatives":[],"none_reason":null},{"match":"none","rt_id":null,"alternatives":[],"general":false,"none_reason":"no_rt_on_date"},{"match":"suggest","rt_id":1,"alternatives":[2],"none_reason":null},{"match":"none","rt_id":null,"alternatives":[],"general":false,"none_reason":"no_rt_on_date"},{"match":"sure","rt_id":4,"alternatives":[],"none_reason":null},{"match":"none","rt_id":null,"alternatives":[],"general":true,"none_reason":"no_plate"},{"match":"none","rt_id":null,"alternatives":[],"general":false,"none_reason":"unknown_plate"},{"match":"none","rt_id":null,"alternatives":[],"general":true,"none_reason":"general_fee"}];
const TODAY_PREF = {"match":"suggest","rt_id":2,"alternatives":[1],"none_reason":null};

test('model 1 only: every answer is today\'s, byte for byte (an hour on the line changes nothing)', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(L.map((l) => matchRoundTrip(l, RTS, RULES)), TODAY);
  assert.deepEqual(matchRoundTrip({ plate: 'XX9', truck_id: 7, service_date: '2026-10-14' }, RTS, RULES), TODAY_PREF);
});

// Two v5 cycles of truck 9 on Wednesday 14/10: A Tue 06:00 -> Wed 06:00, B Wed 14:00 -> Thu 20:00 (Athens, UTC+3).
const A = { id: 21, truck_id: 9, status: 'planned', model: 2, date_start: '2026-10-13', date_end: '2026-10-14',
  start_at: '2026-10-13T03:00:00+00:00', end_at: '2026-10-14T03:00:00+00:00' };
const B = { id: 22, truck_id: 9, status: 'planned', model: 2, date_start: '2026-10-14', date_end: '2026-10-15',
  start_at: '2026-10-14T11:00:00+00:00', end_at: '2026-10-15T17:00:00+00:00' };
const fuel = (time, date = '2026-10-14') => ({ plate: 'XX3', truck_id: 9, service_date: date, ...(time ? { time } : {}) });

test('tech F3: Wednesday 06:00 end, 14:00 start, fuel at 10:00 -> review with both, never a pick', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(matchRoundTrip(fuel('10:00'), [A, B], []),
    { match: 'none', rt_id: null, alternatives: [21, 22], general: false, none_reason: 'review_cycles' });
});

test('an hour inside one window is that cycle (the end is open: 06:00 is the next one\'s, not A\'s)', () => {
  assert.deepEqual(matchRoundTrip(fuel('05:59'), [A, B], []), { match: 'sure', rt_id: 21, alternatives: [], none_reason: null });
  assert.deepEqual(matchRoundTrip(fuel('15:30'), [A, B], []), { match: 'sure', rt_id: 22, alternatives: [], none_reason: null });
  assert.equal(matchRoundTrip(fuel('06:00'), [A, B], []).none_reason, 'review_cycles');
});

test('no hour: two cycles that day are a review; rt_pref of the plate never decides between cycles', () => {
  const r = matchRoundTrip(fuel(null), [A, B], [{ kind: 'rt_pref', key: 'XX3', value: { rt_id: 22 } }]);
  assert.deepEqual(r, { match: 'none', rt_id: null, alternatives: [21, 22], general: false, none_reason: 'review_cycles' });
});

test('one v5 cycle: sure without an hour, review with an hour outside its window', () => {
  assert.equal(matchRoundTrip(fuel(null), [B], []).rt_id, 22);
  assert.deepEqual(matchRoundTrip(fuel('09:00'), [B], []).alternatives, [22]);
  assert.equal(matchRoundTrip(fuel('09:00'), [B], []).match, 'none');
});

test('a v5 cycle and a trip of today\'s model the same day: review, even with the hour inside the cycle', () => {
  const V1 = { id: 30, truck_id: 9, status: 'planned', date_start: '2026-10-14', date_end: '2026-10-16' };
  assert.deepEqual(matchRoundTrip(fuel('15:00'), [B, V1], []).alternatives.sort(), [22, 30]);
  assert.equal(matchRoundTrip(fuel('15:00'), [B, V1], []).none_reason, 'review_cycles');
});

test('the Athens clock, not an offset: on 25/10/2026 (summer time ends) the window 03:00-04:00 holds 03:30, not 04:30', () => {
  const C = { id: 40, truck_id: 9, status: 'planned', model: 2, date_start: '2026-10-25', date_end: '2026-10-25',
    start_at: '2026-10-25T00:00:00+00:00', end_at: '2026-10-25T02:00:00+00:00' };   // 03:00 summer -> 04:00 winter
  assert.equal(matchRoundTrip(fuel('03:30', '2026-10-25'), [C], []).match, 'sure');
  assert.equal(matchRoundTrip(fuel('04:30', '2026-10-25'), [C], []).match, 'none');
});

test('a cancelled cycle is never a candidate', () => {
  assert.equal(matchRoundTrip(fuel('15:00'), [{ ...B, status: 'cancelled' }], []).none_reason, 'no_rt_on_date');
});
```

Run: `node --test worker/test/dkv-match-cycles.test.mjs 2>&1 | grep -E '^ℹ (pass|fail)'`
Expected: **FAIL** (`ℹ pass 2`, `ℹ fail 6`: το πρώτο test, οι σημερινές απαντήσεις, και το «cancelled» περνούν ήδη).

```diff
--- a/worker/src/import-rules.mjs
+++ b/worker/src/import-rules.mjs
@@ -377,7 +377,29 @@
   return Math.round((new Date(hi) - new Date(lo)) / 86400000) + 1;
 }
 
-// rts: [{id, truck_id, status, date_start, date_end}], already filtered to
+// 073 / E10 (spec 5): a v5 cycle (ct_round_trips.model = 2) has an exact window, start_at..end_at. A line that
+// carries its hour (the parser's `time`, HH:MM) is matched to a v5 cycle only INSIDE that window - two cycles of
+// one truck on the same day (Wednesday 06:00 end, Wednesday 14:00 start) are told apart by the hour, never by the
+// +-1 day tolerance of today's round trips. [D-DKV-1] the hour is read on the Athens clock (draft; owner decision).
+// Intl with a time zone, not an offset: the Athens offset changes twice a year.
+const ATHENS_CLOCK = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('sv-SE', { timeZone: 'Europe/Athens', year: 'numeric', month: '2-digit',
+  day: '2-digit', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit', hourCycle: 'h23' });
+function athensStamp(iso) {   // '2026-10-14T03:00:00+00:00' -> '2026-10-14 06:00'
+  if (!iso) return null;
+  const d = new Date(iso);
+  return Number.isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : ATHENS_CLOCK.format(d);
+}
+function lineStamp(line) {    // service_date + time -> '2026-10-14 10:05', or null when the line has no hour
+  const m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})/.exec(line.time || '');
+  if (!line.service_date || !m) return null;
+  return `${line.service_date} ${m[1].padStart(2, '0')}:${m[2]}`;
+}
+function inCycleWindow(rt, stamp) {
+  const a = athensStamp(rt.start_at), b = athensStamp(rt.end_at);
+  return a != null && b != null && a <= stamp && stamp < b;
+}
+
+// rts: [{id, truck_id, status, date_start, date_end, model?, start_at?, end_at?}], already filtered to
 // status <> cancelled by the caller's DB query is fine, but this re-checks
 // status too — a caller that forgets the filter must not silently match a
 // cancelled round trip.
@@ -402,6 +424,23 @@
   const candidates = (rts || []).filter(
     (rt) => rt.status !== 'cancelled' && rt.truck_id === line.truck_id && dateOverlaps(line, rt)
   );
+  // 073 / E10: when a v5 cycle may be meant. With the hour, the candidates are the trips of today's model and the v5
+  // cycles whose window holds the hour; one = sure. An hour outside every v5 window of that day (the truck between
+  // two cycles: Wednesday 06:00 end, 14:00 start, fuel at 10:00) is not guessed either. [D-DKV-2] Two or more
+  // candidates with a v5 cycle among them, or an hour between cycles, are a review: none, the candidates listed -
+  // the accountant picks, the import never does (tech F3: "never a random choice"; rt_pref is a rule of a plate,
+  // not of a cycle). With no v5 candidate, today's rule below runs unchanged (spec: today's trips as before).
+  if (candidates.some((rt) => rt.model === 2)) {
+    const review = (list) => ({ match: 'none', rt_id: null, alternatives: list.map((c) => c.id), general: false, none_reason: 'review_cycles' });
+    const stamp = lineStamp(line);
+    if (stamp) {
+      const inside = candidates.filter((rt) => rt.model === 2 && inCycleWindow(rt, stamp));
+      if (!inside.length) return review(candidates);
+      const pool = candidates.filter((rt) => rt.model !== 2).concat(inside);
+      return pool.length === 1 ? { match: 'sure', rt_id: pool[0].id, alternatives: [], none_reason: null } : review(pool);
+    }
+    return candidates.length === 1 ? { match: 'sure', rt_id: candidates[0].id, alternatives: [], none_reason: null } : review(candidates);
+  }
   if (!candidates.length) return { match: 'none', rt_id: null, alternatives: [], general: false, none_reason: 'no_rt_on_date' };
   if (candidates.length === 1) return { match: 'sure', rt_id: candidates[0].id, alternatives: [], none_reason: null };
 
```

`worker/test/costs-rt-ended.test.mjs`:
```js
// 073 / E21 (spec 2.3): GET /costs/rt gives a v5 cycle (model 2) its end as ONE rule - v_rt_cycle.ended (the Athens
// day of its last stop has passed) - in a field of its own, `ended`. Its status stays what the base says (planned or
// cancelled for ever, 071). A failed read is said (ended: null, ended_unknown: true), never "still running". A trip
// of today's model (model 1) comes back exactly as before: no `ended`, no extra read.
// Against worker/src/index.js itself, PostgREST stubbed via fetch (the harness of costs-rt-filter.test.mjs).
import { test, beforeEach, afterEach } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { register } from 'node:module';

const HERE = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const SRC_PATH = path.resolve(HERE, '../src/index.js');
register('data:text/javascript,' + encodeURIComponent(`
export async function resolve(spec, ctx, next) {
  if (spec === '@cloudflare/puppeteer') return { url: 'data:text/javascript,export default {}', shortCircuit: true };
  return next(spec, ctx);
}`));
const { default: worker } = await import(pathToFileURL(SRC_PATH).href);

const ORIGIN = 'https://dimitrispetras21-del.github.io';
const env = { JWT_SECRET: 'test-secret-not-real', ALLOWED_ORIGIN: ORIGIN, SUPABASE_URL: 'https://db.invalid', SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY: 'svc-test' };
const ctx = { waitUntil() {} };
const b64u = (b) => Buffer.from(b).toString('base64url');
async function token(role, sub = 'tester') {
  const input = `${b64u(JSON.stringify({ alg: 'HS256', typ: 'JWT' }))}.${b64u(JSON.stringify({ sub, role, exp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + 600 }))}`;
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', new TextEncoder().encode(env.JWT_SECRET), { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']);
  return `${input}.${b64u(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, new TextEncoder().encode(input))))}`;
}
async function call(role, pathAndQuery) {
  const req = new Request(`https://w.invalid${pathAndQuery}`, { method: 'GET', headers: { Origin: ORIGIN, Authorization: `Bearer ${await token(role)}` } });
  const res = await worker.fetch(req, env, ctx);
  const text = await res.text();
  let json = null;
  try { json = JSON.parse(text); } catch {}
  return { status: res.status, json, text };
}

const TRIPS = [
  { id: 1, model: 1, status: 'closed', date_start: '2026-10-12', date_end: '2026-10-14' },
  { id: 2, model: 2, status: 'planned', date_start: '2026-10-13', date_end: '2026-10-14' },
  { id: 3, model: 2, status: 'planned', date_start: '2026-10-30', date_end: '2026-10-31' },
];
const json = (rows, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(rows), { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
let calls; let cycleFails; let trips;
const realConsole = { log: console.log, error: console.error, warn: console.warn };
beforeEach(() => {
  console.log = console.error = console.warn = () => {};
  calls = []; cycleFails = false; trips = TRIPS;
  globalThis.fetch = async (url, init = {}) => {
    const u = new URL(url);
    const table = u.pathname.replace(/^\/rest\/v1\//, '');
    calls.push({ table, params: u.searchParams });
    if (table === 'ct_round_trips') return json(trips.map((r) => ({ ...r, ct_rt_legs: [] })));
    if (table === 'dl_v_rt_route') return json([]);
    if (table === 'dl_entries') return json([]);
    if (table === 'v_rt_cycle') {
      if (cycleFails) return json({ message: 'boom' }, 500);
      return json([{ rt_id: 2, ended: true }, { rt_id: 3, ended: false }]);
    }
    throw new Error(`unmocked fetch: ${table}`);
  };
});
afterEach(() => { Object.assign(console, realConsole); });

test('a v5 cycle carries ended from v_rt_cycle; its status is untouched', async () => {
  const r = await call('dispatcher', '/costs/rt');
  assert.equal(r.status, 200, r.text);
  const by = Object.fromEntries(r.json.records.map((x) => [x.id, x]));
  assert.equal(by[2].ended, true);
  assert.equal(by[3].ended, false);
  assert.equal(by[2].status, 'planned');
  const cyc = calls.find((c) => c.table === 'v_rt_cycle');
  assert.equal(cyc.params.get('rt_id'), 'in.(2,3)', 'one read, only the v5 ids');
  assert.equal(cyc.params.get('select'), 'rt_id,ended', 'no money, no vehicle: only the end');
});

test('a trip of today\'s model is returned as before: no ended field', async () => {
  const r = await call('owner', '/costs/rt');
  const one = r.json.records.find((x) => x.id === 1);
  assert.equal('ended' in one, false);
  assert.equal('ended_unknown' in one, false);
});

test('no v5 cycle in the page: no v_rt_cycle read at all', async () => {
  trips = [TRIPS[0]];
  const r = await call('owner', '/costs/rt');
  assert.equal(r.status, 200, r.text);
  assert.equal(calls.filter((c) => c.table === 'v_rt_cycle').length, 0);
});

test('v_rt_cycle fails: the list still loads, and every v5 cycle says it does not know', async () => {
  cycleFails = true;
  const r = await call('owner', '/costs/rt');
  assert.equal(r.status, 200, r.text);
  for (const x of r.json.records.filter((y) => y.model === 2)) {
    assert.equal(x.ended, null);
    assert.equal(x.ended_unknown, true);
  }
  assert.equal('ended' in r.json.records.find((x) => x.id === 1), false);
});
```

```js
// live row per international stop and event) answers 409 in Greek instead of a bare 500. Rows without piece_leg_id
// behave exactly as before.

register('data:text/javascript,' + encodeURIComponent(`
export async function resolve(spec, ctx, next) {
  if (spec === '@cloudflare/puppeteer') return { url: 'data:text/javascript,export default {}', shortCircuit: true };
  return next(spec, ctx);
}`));
const { default: worker } = await import(pathToFileURL(SRC_PATH).href);

const env = { JWT_SECRET: 'test-secret-not-real', ALLOWED_ORIGIN: ORIGIN, SUPABASE_URL: 'https://db.invalid', SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY: 'svc-test' };

  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw', new TextEncoder().encode(env.JWT_SECRET), { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' }, false, ['sign']);
  return `${input}.${b64u(new Uint8Array(await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, new TextEncoder().encode(input))))}`;
}
async function call(role, pathAndQuery, method = 'GET', body) {

  const res = await worker.fetch(req, env, ctx);

  { id: 3, code: 'PM-3', status: 'confirmed', event_type: 'DELIVERY', counterparty_type: 'CLIENT', client_id: 5, location_id: 6, movement_date: '2026-10-14', taken: 10, given: 10, piece_leg_id: 70 },
];
const json = (rows, status = 200) => new Response(JSON.stringify(rows), { status, headers: { 'content-type': 'application/json' } });
let calls; let legsFail; let insertError;

beforeEach(() => {

    if (table === 'piece_legs') {
      if (legsFail) return json({ message: 'boom' }, 500);
      return json([{ id: 70, shipment_pieces: { shipments: { nat_order_id: 501 } } }]);
    }
    if (table === 'pl_movements' && method === 'PATCH') return json([{ ...ROWS[0], ...JSON.parse(init.body) }]);
    if (table === 'pl_movements' && method === 'POST') {
      if (insertError) return json(insertError, 409);
      return json([{ id: 3, ...JSON.parse(init.body) }], 201);
    }
    if (table === 'pl_movements' && method === 'DELETE') return new Response(null, { status: 204 });
    if (table === 'audit_log') return json([], 201);
    if (['clients', 'locations', 'orders', 'order_stops'].includes(table)) return json([{ id: 5 }]);
    throw new Error(`unmocked fetch: ${method} ${table}`);
  };
});
afterEach(() => { Object.assign(console, realConsole); });

test('GET: a row of the plan names its national order; a manual row is as before', async () => {
  const r = await call('accountant', '/pallets/movements');
  assert.equal(r.status, 200, r.text);
  const by = Object.fromEntries(r.json.records.map((x) => [x.id, x]));
  assert.equal(by[1].nat_order_id, 501);
  assert.equal('nat_order_id' in by[2], false);
  const legs = calls.find((c) => c.table === 'piece_legs');
  assert.equal(legs.params.get('id'), 'in.(70)', 'one read for the page');
});

test('GET: the lookup fails - the list loads and says it does not know', async () => {
  legsFail = true;
  const r = await call('accountant', '/pallets/movements');
  assert.equal(r.status, 200, r.text);
  const one = r.json.records.find((x) => x.id === 1);
  assert.equal(one.nat_order_id, null);
  assert.equal(one.nat_order_unknown, true);
});

test('PATCH: on a row of the plan only given and the sheet change', async () => {
  const ok = await call('warehouse', '/pallets/movements/1', 'PATCH', { given: 8, sheet_source: 'MANUAL' });
  assert.equal(ok.status, 200, ok.text);
  // taken is the plan's (the piece's pallets): corrected in the plan, or the next change of the leg would undo it
  for (const body of [{ movement_date: '2026-10-15' }, { client_rec: 'recX' }, { event_type: 'DELIVERY' }, { given: 8, location_rec: 'recY' }, { taken: 9 }]) {
    calls = [];
    const r = await call('owner', '/pallets/movements/1', 'PATCH', body);
    assert.equal(r.status, 409, JSON.stringify(body));
    assert.equal(calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'PATCH').length, 0, 'nothing written');
  }
  const manual = await call('owner', '/pallets/movements/2', 'PATCH', { movement_date: '2026-10-15' });
  assert.equal(manual.status, 200, 'a manual row edits as before');
});

test('DELETE: a row of the plan is refused; a manual pending row is deleted as before', async () => {
  const r = await call('owner', '/pallets/movements/1', 'DELETE');
  assert.equal(r.status, 409, r.text);
  assert.equal(calls.filter((c) => c.method === 'DELETE').length, 0);
  const m = await call('owner', '/pallets/movements/2', 'DELETE');
  assert.equal(m.status, 200, m.text);
});

test('reverse: the replacement of a row of the plan stays the leg\'s row (only given, sheet, notes from the body)', async () => {
  const r = await call('accountant', '/pallets/movements/3/reverse', 'POST',
    { reason: 'TEST', replacement: { given: 8, client_rec: 'recX', movement_date: '2026-10-20', event_type: 'LOADING' } });
  assert.equal(r.status, 200, r.text);
  const ins = calls.find((c) => c.method === 'POST' && c.table === 'pl_movements');
  const { partner_id: _p, ...got } = ins.body;   // null on a real row, absent in this mock: not the point here
  assert.deepEqual(got, { movement_date: '2026-10-14', counterparty_type: 'CLIENT', client_id: 5, location_id: 6,
    event_type: 'DELIVERY', taken: 10, given: 8, reversal_of: 3, created_by: 'tester', piece_leg_id: 70 });
  const patchIdx = calls.findIndex((c) => c.method === 'PATCH'), insIdx = calls.findIndex((c) => c.method === 'POST' && c.table === 'pl_movements');
  assert.ok(patchIdx >= 0 && patchIdx < insIdx, 'the old row is reversed first (pl_mov_leg_once counts live rows only)');
});

test('POST: the second live row of a stop and event is a 409 in Greek, other refusals as before', async () => {
  insertError = { code: '23505', message: 'duplicate key value violates unique constraint "pl_mov_stop_once"', details: 'Key (order_stop_id, event_type)=(4, DELIVERY) already exists.' };
  const body = { movement_date: '2026-10-14', counterparty_type: 'CLIENT', client_rec: 'recC', event_type: 'DELIVERY', taken: 33, given: 33, order_stop_rec: 'recS' };
  const r = await call('dispatcher', '/pallets/movements', 'POST', body);
  assert.equal(r.status, 409, r.text);
  assert.match(r.text, /Υπάρχει ήδη κίνηση παλετών/);
  insertError = { code: '23505', message: 'duplicate key value violates unique constraint "pl_movements_code_key"' };
  const other = await call('dispatcher', '/pallets/movements', 'POST', body);
  assert.equal(other.status, 500, 'any other refusal: as before');
});
```

`worker/test/worker-guard-f3.test.mjs`:
```js
// worker/tools/worker-guard.mjs --f3 (national v5 F3): the deploy guard knows the F3 marker "v_rt_cycle" (073 /
// E21: GET /costs/rt reads the end of a v5 cycle). Before the F3 deploy the live script must NOT have it (--f3 0),
// after it must (--f3 1). Without --f3 the tool answers exactly as in F2 (its own test, worker-guard.test.mjs).
import { test } from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync, mkdtempSync } from 'node:fs';
import { tmpdir } from 'node:os';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import path from 'node:path';

const here = path.dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
const TOOL = path.join(here, '..', 'tools', 'worker-guard.mjs');
const SRC = path.join(here, '..', 'src', 'index.js');
const src = readFileSync(SRC, 'utf8');
const plan = src.includes('var PLAN_PERMS = {') ? '1' : '0';   // on deploy/worker-plan: 1 (F2 is in the chain)
const dir = mkdtempSync(path.join(tmpdir(), 'worker-guard-f3-'));
const run = (file, ...args) => {
  const r = spawnSync(process.execPath, [TOOL, file, ...args], { encoding: 'utf8' });
  return { code: r.status, out: r.stdout + r.stderr };
};

test('the F3 source passes with --f3 1 and fails with --f3 0, naming the marker', () => {
  let r = run(SRC, '--plan', plan, '--f3', '1');
  assert.equal(r.code, 0, r.out);
  assert.match(r.out, new RegExp(`GUARD OK \\(\\d+ markers, plan=${plan}, f3=1\\)`));
  r = run(SRC, '--plan', plan, '--f3', '0');
  assert.equal(r.code, 1, r.out);
  assert.match(r.out, /STOP {2}"v_rt_cycle" \(F3, E21\) = 1/);
});

test('a script without the F3 code: --f3 0 passes, --f3 1 stops', () => {
  const f = path.join(dir, 'f2-only.js');
  writeFileSync(f, src.split('"v_rt_cycle"').join('"v_rt_cycle_gone"'));
  assert.equal(run(f, '--plan', plan, '--f3', '0').code, 0);
  const r = run(f, '--plan', plan, '--f3', '1');
  assert.equal(r.code, 1, r.out);
  assert.match(r.out, /STOP {2}"v_rt_cycle" \(F3, E21\) = 0/);
});

test('bad --f3 usage → exit 2; without --f3 the F2 answer is unchanged', () => {
  assert.equal(run(SRC, '--plan', plan, '--f3', '2').code, 2);
  assert.equal(run(SRC, '--plan', plan, '--f4', '1').code, 2);
  assert.equal(run(SRC, '--plan', plan, '--f3', '1', 'x').code, 2);
  const r = run(SRC, '--plan', plan);
  assert.doesNotMatch(r.out, /v_rt_cycle/);
});
```

| ID | Τι μετρά | Σοβ. | Ουρά | Σήμερα | Πότε κοκκινίζει (σενάριο PGlite) |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| B-78 | παραγγελία με ζωντανό φορτίο του σημερινού μοντέλου **και** αποστολή v5: VS με Direct NL, εθνική με NL ή γραμμή groupage (spec §8 «ίδια παραγγελία») | P1 | όχι | 0 | VS με γέφυρα + NL **και** εθνική με NL (= 2) |
| B-79 💶 M-4 | Σ μεριδίων X ανά VS ≠ X της παραγγελίας (ανοχή 0,01· M-4 β, owner 24/10) | P1 | όχι | 0 | διπλό μερίδιο X ή λάθος παρονομαστής (όχι πια «X στις ρυθμίσεις 700»: με M-4 β η αλλαγή ρύθμισης δεν κοκκινίζει· το scenario test του 073b αλλάζει στο Task 0) |
| B-80 💶 M-5 | Σ μεριδίων τιμής ≠ τιμή εθνικής (ανοχή 0,01) | P2 | όχι | 0 | **ποτέ** με M-3 β: απενεργοποιημένος ως το L (M-5 δ, owner 24/10) |
| B-81 | κομμάτι που δεν έφυγε στην ώρα του (`v_piece_state.overdue`) | P2 | ναι | 0 | εθνική 50 ημέρες πίσω |
| B-82 | άδειος κύκλος v5 (όχι ακυρωμένος) | P2 | όχι | 0 | `plan_rt_create` μόνο του |
| B-83 | τμήμα PARTNER χωρίς κόμιστρο και χωρίς τιμολόγιο **του ίδιου** συνεργάτη (Q6-1· διαβάζει την `ct_v_rt_partner_cost`, τον ίδιο κανόνα με το `partner_rate_missing`) | P2 | ναι | 0 | PARTNER χωρίς rate |

| B-85 | μεικτές θερμοκρασίες ≥ 2 °C στην ίδια πλευρά | P3 | ναι | 0 | 2 °C και 6 °C στον ίδιο κύκλο |

«Ποιος το μαθαίνει» = η ειδοποίηση 048 του ελεγκτή (P1 αμέσως, P2 στη σύνοψη των 17:00), όπως κάθε έλεγχος. Οι P1 (B-78, B-79, B-89) δεν μπορούν να κοκκινίσουν πριν από την πρώτη γραμμή v5.

## Αν σπάσει, πώς θα το μάθουμε

- **Στην εκτέλεση:** κάθε φρουρός και απόδειξη της 073 είναι `RAISE` μέσα σε ένα block: ένα λάθος md5, ένα νούμερο του σήμερα που άλλαξε, ένα `security_invoker` που χάθηκε, ένα grant παραπάνω → τίποτα δεν κρατιέται. Αποτύπωμα πριν/μετά από τον owner.
- **Αργότερα στη βάση:** το `073_plan_money_verify.sql` (views_changed, functions_changed, invoker_lost, open_money, open_helper) και οι 13 έλεγχοι· ο B-54 πιάνει trigger που δεν ανήκει.
- **Στον Worker:** αποτυχία ανάγνωσης = `ended_unknown` / `nat_order_unknown`, ποτέ σιωπηλό «σε εξέλιξη» ή «Χειροκίνητη»· το 409 του `pl_mov_stop_once` / `pl_mov_leg_once` λέει στα ελληνικά ότι υπάρχει ήδη κίνηση· 503 `f3_missing` αν ο Worker μπει πριν από την 072b/073· ο φρουρός του deploy με ένα σημάδι ανά λειτουργία (`"v_rt_cycle"`, `review_cycles`, `pl_mov_stop_once`, `ctPartnerLineCheck`).
- **Στα χρήματα:** B-79/B-80 (μερίδια ≠ σύνολο), B-83 (κόμιστρο που λείπει), B-90 (τιμή που χάθηκε), B-84 (έξοδα που έμειναν σε ακυρωμένο κύκλο).
- **Στις παλέτες:** B-88 (εκκρεμής σε σβησμένη κίνηση), B-89 (δεύτερος συγγραφέας), B-18b (το πλήθος δεν μειώνεται: ο feeder δεν σβήνει ποτέ), B-17 (εκκρεμείς > 7 ημέρες).

## Self-review (έγινε)

- **Κάλυψη master/spec:** Ε6 (D-3, κλάδος + περίπτωση 30), Ε7 (όψη μεριδίων, έσοδο, κόστος συνεργάτη, σημαία· περιπτώσεις 16–25), Ε8 (D-19, περίπτωση 31, B-87, M-7), Ε9 (διαδρομή ανά πλευρά· 26–28), Ε10 (Task 7), Ε11 (feeder, πύλη, ετικέτα, φρουρός διπλής DELIVERY/LOADING· 1–15, 29, Task 9), Ε21 (Task 8), snapshot πριν = μετά (μέσα στο block + αποτύπωμα + PGlite σε δεδομένα), έλεγχοι §8 (13). Αποδοχή του master («γραμμές `dl_v_rt_route`/`ct_v_rt_pnl` ίσες με πριν»): Task 12 βήματα 3–5, Task 15 Step 2.
- **Χρήματα:** κανένας κανόνας χρημάτων ή DKV που δεν τον ορίζει το spec δεν αποφασίστηκε από τον agent (M-1..M-7, DKV-1..3)· ο κώδικας κάθε επιλογής δοκιμάστηκε.
- **Ρόλοι:** οι αλλαγές σε ό,τι μπορεί να κάνει ένας ρόλος (D-9 UNIQUE, D-14 πολιτικές, D-15 grant) είναι αποφάσεις owner (μνήμη 10/10: «ρώτα πριν»).
- **Υπογραφές:** κάθε κλήση της 073 προς την 072 χρησιμοποιεί τις υπογραφές του πίνακα «Εσωτερικοί βοηθοί» της Φ2 (`plan_h_audit` 7 ορίσματα, `plan_h_raise` 2)· ο βοηθός της 073 είναι invoker, owner postgres, `SET search_path = public` (απόδειξη στο PART 5).
- **Κριτική 23/10 (νύχτα), εφαρμοσμένη στον κώδικα και ξανά δοκιμασμένη:** πάνω στην 072 του πλάνου Φ2 μετά τη δεύτερη κριτική του (K-22: άλλο κείμενο σε 3 συναρτήσεις με άγκιστρο, νέα αρχή γραμμής στο `plan_rt_update`)· ένας συγγραφέας ανά πεδίο στις γραμμές παλετών (το `given` του χρήστη μένει, D-5, περίπτωση 33· PATCH χωρίς `taken`· ο αντιλογισμός κρατά την αντικατάσταση στο σκέλος, D-9)· η τιμή εθνικής μία φορά (`natord_home`, περίπτωση 34) και ο B-78 και για εθνικές· M-5 με το όριο του B-80· K-23 (απόφαση 21 της Φ2). Αναφορά: `.superpowers/f3/critique.md`.
- **Ό,τι δεν έτρεξε:** λήψη `live-072.sql`, Worker πάνω στην αλυσίδα Φ2 (contract test, `PLAN_RULE_TEXT`), οι φρουροί 057 της παραγωγής στην περίπτωση 30 (μόνο στον Task 10 με την πραγματική λήψη και στο προαιρετικό βήμα 7 του Task 12).
